[31m+ const ipMap = new Map<string, { n: number; reset: number }>();[39m
[31m+ function rateOk(ip: string) {[39m
[31m+   const now = Date.now();[39m
[31m+   const rec = ipMap.get(ip);[39m
[31m+   if (!rec || now > rec.reset) {[39m
[31m+     ipMap.set(ip, { n: 1, reset: now + 60_000 });[39m
[31m+     return true;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+   if (rec.n >= 20) return false;[39m
[31m+   rec.n++;[39m
[31m+   return true;[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+           policyRequiresManualReview: policyQuote.requiresManualReview,[39m
[31m+           policyRefundAmount: policyQuote.refundAmount,[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+         : null;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const autoRefund = resolveSelfCancelRefundAmount({[39m
[31m+         refundable: selfCancel.refundable,[39m
[31m+         policyRefundAmount: policyQuote.refundAmount,[39m
[31m+         requiresManualReview: false,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 信用卡（含已全額退款、政策 0 元退款）一律自動處理，不進 business review[39m
[31m+       const canAutoHandle = selfCancel.mode === "direct_cancel" ||[39m
[31m+         (selfCancel.isCardOrder && !cardException);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── 信用卡 TECHNICAL EXCEPTION：不取消、不假裝完成、不建立取消審核 ──[39m
[31m+       if (selfCancel.isCardOrder && cardException) {[39m
[31m+         await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+           campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+           order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+           event_type: "note",[39m
[31m+           actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+           note: `self_cancel_technical_exception／reason:${cardException}／${[39m
[31m+             CARD_SELF_CANCEL_EXCEPTION_LABEL[cardException][39m
[31m+           }${reason ? `／旅客原因:${reason}` : ""}`,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         try {[39m
[31m+           await admin.from("notifications").insert({[39m
[31m+             accommodation_id: accIdForCancel,[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             type: "payment_exception",[39m
[31m+             title: "⚠️ 團購信用卡自助取消：系統無法自動處理",[39m
[31m+             description:[39m
[31m+               `訂單 ${order.order_number} 的旅客已提出取消，但${CARD_SELF_CANCEL_EXCEPTION_LABEL[cardException]}，` +[39m
[31m+               `系統未執行退款也未取消訂單。這是技術／設定異常，請至「金流稽核」修正後完成處理（技術異常，非人工同意取消）。`,[39m
[31m+             is_read: false,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+           console.error("[group-buy-order-lookup] technical exception alert failed", e);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return json({[39m
[31m+           success: true,[39m
[31m+           processing: true,[39m
[31m+           mode: "technical_processing",[39m
[31m+           message: "系統正在處理您的取消，完成後將另行通知。",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (canAutoHandle) {[39m
[31m+         const nowIso = new Date().toISOString();[39m
[31m+         const cancelledAtText = formatTaipei(new Date(nowIso));[39m
[31m+         const paid = selfCancel.paid;[39m
[31m+         const alreadyRefunded = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).refunded_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+         const alreadyRefundFee = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).refund_fee_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+         const isAtm = String((order as any).payment_method ?? "") === "atm" || !!(order as any).atm_vacc_no;[39m
[31m+         /** 信用卡且政策計算結果 0 元 → 不呼叫 TapPay，直接取消 */[39m
[31m+         const zeroRefundByPolicy = selfCancel.isCardOrder && paid > 0 && autoRefund.refundAmount <= 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // ── B：refund-first，TapPay 失敗即中止，訂單維持未取消 ──[39m
[31m+         let refundAmount = 0;[39m
[31m+         let refundRecTradeId: string | null = null;[39m
[31m+         if (selfCancel.isCardOrder && autoRefund.refundAmount > 0) {[39m
[31m+           const settle = await getPaymentAdapter(order as any).refundPayment({[39m
[31m+             order: { ...(order as any), id: order.id },[39m
[31m+             amount: autoRefund.refundAmount,[39m
[31m+             note: reason || "旅客自助取消",[39m
[31m+             supabase: admin,[39m
[31m+             triggerSource: "group_buy_self_cancel",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (!settle.ok) {[39m
[31m+             await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+               campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+               order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+               event_type: "note",[39m
[31m+               actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+               note: `self_cancel_refund_failed／${settle.error ?? "unknown"}／${settle.message ?? ""}`,[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+             return json({[39m
[31m+               error: settle.needsReconciliation[39m
[31m+                 ? "退款結果尚待金流確認，請稍後再試或聯繫客服，我們會盡快為您處理。"[39m
[31m+                 : (settle.message ?? "目前無法完成自動退款，請聯繫客服協助處理。"),[39m
[31m+             }, 400);[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           refundAmount = autoRefund.refundAmount;[39m
[31m+           refundRecTradeId = settle.recTradeId ?? null;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const totalRefunded = alreadyRefunded + refundAmount;[39m
[31m+         const totalSettled = totalRefunded + alreadyRefundFee;[39m
[31m+         // 政策 0 元退款＝金流已「依規則處理完畢」，不得標記為退款處理中（none = 不進行退款）[39m
[31m+         const refundStatus = paid <= 0 || zeroRefundByPolicy[39m
[31m+           ? "none"[39m
[31m+           : refundStatusAfterRefund(paid, totalSettled);[39m
[31m+         const paymentStatusPatch = totalSettled <= 0[39m
[31m+           ? {}[39m
[31m+           : { payment_status: totalSettled >= paid ? "refunded" : "partial_refunded" };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const noteLines = [[39m
[31m+           order.notes ?? "",[39m
[31m+           `【取消】${cancelledAtText} 由旅客自助取消（團購訂單查詢頁）`,[39m
[31m+           refundAmount > 0[39m
[31m+             ? `退款處理：已完成原信用卡退刷 NT$${refundAmount.toLocaleString("en-US")}（TapPay${[39m
[31m+               refundRecTradeId ? ` ${refundRecTradeId}` : ""[39m
[31m+             }）`[39m
[31m+             : zeroRefundByPolicy[39m
[31m+             ? "退款處理：依取消政策，本次退款金額 NT$0（未呼叫退款 API，已收款金額保留為金流事實）"[39m
[31m+             : paid > 0[39m
[31m+             ? "退款處理：依取消政策不予退款"[39m
[31m+             : "退款處理：未收款，不需退款",[39m
[31m+           `取消政策：${policyQuote.policy}（${policyQuote.policyReason}）／距入住 ${policyQuote.daysBefore} 天／比例 ${policyQuote.refundPercent}%／已付 NT$${[39m
[31m+             paid.toLocaleString("en-US")[39m
[31m+           }／本次退款 NT$${refundAmount.toLocaleString("en-US")}`,[39m
[31m+           reason ? `原因：${reason}` : null,[39m
[31m+         ].filter(Boolean).join("\n");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 併發保護：付款狀態若在此期間變動（例如遲到入帳）則中止，改走人工[39m
[31m+         const { data: affected, error: uErr } = await admin.rpc("update_order_with_context", {[39m
[31m+           p_order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+           p_patch: {[39m
[31m+             order_status: "cancelled",[39m
[31m+             cancelled_at: (order as any).cancelled_at ?? nowIso,[39m
[31m+             refunded_amount: totalRefunded,[39m
[31m+             refunded_at: refundAmount > 0 ? nowIso : (order as any).refunded_at,[39m
[31m+             // 供後台「同步 TapPay 退款狀態」沿用同一組 provider 交易編號做對帳（不另建 sync 流程）[39m
[31m+             ...(refundRecTradeId ? { refund_rec_trade_id: refundRecTradeId } : {}),[39m
[31m+             refund_status: refundStatus,[39m
[31m+             ...paymentStatusPatch,[39m
[31m+             notes: noteLines,[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+           p_source: "group_buy_guest_cancel",[39m
[31m+           p_reason: reason || null,[39m
[31m+           p_actor_label: (order as any).guest_name ?? null,[39m
[31m+           p_actor_type: "system",[39m
[31m+           p_expect: { payment_status: order.payment_status },[39m
[31m+           p_not: { order_status: "cancelled" },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         if (uErr) throw uErr;[39m
[31m+         if (!affected || Number(affected) === 0) {[39m
[31m+           return json({ error: "訂單狀態剛剛有更新，請重新查詢後再試一次。" }, 409);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 分潤作廢[39m
[31m+         const { data: commissionRow } = await admin[39m
[31m+           .from("group_buy_commissions")[39m
[31m+           .select("id")[39m
[31m+           .eq("order_id", order.id)[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+         if (commissionRow?.id) {[39m
[31m+           await admin.from("group_buy_commissions").update({ status: "void" }).eq("id", commissionRow.id);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert([[39m
[31m+           {[39m
[31m+             campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             event_type: "cancelled",[39m
[31m+             actor_label: order.guest_name,[39m
[31m+             note: [[39m
[31m+               reason ? `旅客自助取消訂單：${reason}` : "旅客自助取消訂單",[39m
[31m+               `退款狀態 ${refundStatus}`,[39m
[31m+             ].join("／"),[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+           ...(refundAmount > 0[39m
[31m+             ? [{[39m
[31m+               campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+               order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+               event_type: "refunded",[39m
[31m+               amount: refundAmount,[39m
[31m+               actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+               note: [[39m
[31m+                 "退款方式：原信用卡退刷（TapPay）",[39m
[31m+                 refundRecTradeId ? `交易編號 ${refundRecTradeId}` : null,[39m
[31m+                 `本次退款 NT$${refundAmount.toLocaleString("en-US")}`,[39m
[31m+                 `累計實退 NT$${totalRefunded.toLocaleString("en-US")}`,[39m
[31m+                 `已收 NT$${paid.toLocaleString("en-US")}`,[39m
[31m+               ].filter(Boolean).join("／"),[39m
[31m+             }][39m
[31m+             : []),[39m
[31m+         ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         await admin.from("audit_logs").insert({[39m
[31m+           accommodation_id: accIdForCancel,[39m
[31m+           user_id: null,[39m
[31m+           user_email: order.guest_email ?? null,[39m
[31m+           action_type: "cancel_order",[39m
[31m+           action_category: "group_buy",[39m
[31m+           entity_type: "order",[39m
[31m+           entity_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+           entity_name: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+           details: {[39m
[31m+             source: "group_buy_self_cancel",[39m
[31m+             mode: selfCancel.mode,[39m
[31m+             paid_amount: paid,[39m
[31m+             refund_amount: refundAmount,[39m
[31m+             total_refunded_amount: totalRefunded,[39m
[31m+             refund_status: refundStatus,[39m
[31m+             rec_trade_id: refundRecTradeId,[39m
[31m+             policy: policyQuote.policy,[39m
[31m+             policy_reason: policyQuote.policyReason,[39m
[31m+             policy_refund_percent: policyQuote.refundPercent,[39m
[31m+             operator: "guest",[39m
[31m+             timestamp: nowIso,[39m
[31m+             reason: reason || null,[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 通知（寄信失敗不影響取消結果）[39m
[31m+         try {[39m
[31m+           const { data: accRow } = accIdForCancel[39m
[31m+             ? await admin.from("accommodation_info").select("name").eq("id", accIdForCancel).maybeSingle()[39m
[31m+             : { data: null as any };[39m
[31m+           const baseEmailOrder = buildEmailOrder({[39m
[31m+             order,[39m
[31m+             campaign: campaign ?? { title: "團購活動", deposit_percentage: depositPct, payment_deadline_hours: 72 },[39m
[31m+             accommodationName: (accRow as any)?.name ?? null,[39m
[31m+             packageName: null,[39m
[31m+             frontendOrigin: typeof body.origin === "string" ? body.origin : null,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           const emailOrder = {[39m
[31m+             ...baseEmailOrder,[39m
[31m+             isCardOrder: selfCancel.isCardOrder,[39m
[31m+             paymentMethodText: paymentMethodLabel(order as any),[39m
[31m+             refundChannelText: refundChannelLabel(selfCancel.isCardOrder),[39m
[31m+           } as any;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const guestMail = await sendGroupBuyEmail([39m
[31m+             order.guest_email,[39m
[31m+             renderOrderCancelled(emailOrder, {[39m
[31m+               cancelledAtText,[39m
[31m+               paidAmount: paid,[39m
[31m+               refundedTotal: totalRefunded,[39m
[31m+               refundFeeTotal: alreadyRefundFee,[39m
[31m+               refundStatus,[39m
[31m+               reason: reason || null,[39m
[31m+               atmNoTransferNotice: isAtm && paid <= 0,[39m
[31m+               zeroRefundByPolicy,[39m
[31m+             }),[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+             campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             event_type: guestMail.ok ? "email_sent" : "email_failed",[39m
[31m+             actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+             note: `recipient_type:guest／template_type:guest_self_cancelled${[39m
[31m+               guestMail.ok ? "／recipient_count:1" : `／reason:${guestMail.reason ?? "unknown"}`[39m
[31m+             }`,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const { emails: venueEmails, source: venueSource } = await resolveOrderNotifyEmails(admin, {[39m
[31m+             accommodationId: accIdForCancel,[39m
[31m+             campaignId: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (venueEmails.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+             const venueMail = await sendGroupBuyEmail([39m
[31m+               venueEmails,[39m
[31m+               renderVenueOrderSelfCancelled([39m
[31m+                 {[39m
[31m+                   ...emailOrder,[39m
[31m+                   guestPhone: order.guest_phone ?? null,[39m
[31m+                   guestEmail: order.guest_email ?? null,[39m
[31m+                   adminUrl: buildAdminGroupBuyUrl({[39m
[31m+                     accommodationId: accIdForCancel,[39m
[31m+                     campaignId: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+                     orderNumber: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+                   }),[39m
[31m+                 },[39m
[31m+                 {[39m
[31m+                   cancelledAtText,[39m
[31m+                   reason: reason || null,[39m
[31m+                   paidAmount: paid,[39m
[31m+                   refundedAmount: refundAmount,[39m
[31m+                   refundStatus,[39m
[31m+                   refundChannelText: selfCancel.isCardOrder ? "原信用卡退刷（TapPay）" : "未收款，不需退款",[39m
[31m+                 },[39m
[31m+               ),[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+             await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+               campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+               order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+               event_type: venueMail.ok ? "email_sent" : "email_failed",[39m
[31m+               actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+               note: `recipient_type:venue／template_type:venue_self_cancelled／recipient_count:${venueEmails.length}／source:${venueSource}${[39m
[31m+                 venueMail.ok ? "" : `／reason:${venueMail.reason ?? "unknown"}`[39m
[31m+               }`,[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         } catch (notifyErr) {[39m
[31m+           console.error("[group-buy-order-lookup] self-cancel notify threw", notifyErr);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         return json({[39m
[31m+           success: true,[39m
[31m+           cancelled: true,[39m
[31m+           mode: selfCancel.mode,[39m
[31m+           refundAmount,[39m
[31m+           refundedTotal: totalRefunded,[39m
[31m+           refundStatus,[39m
[31m+           zeroRefundByPolicy,[39m
[31m+           message: refundAmount > 0[39m
[31m+             ? "訂單已取消，退款已送回原付款信用卡（實際入帳依發卡銀行作業時間，約 3～7 個工作天）。"[39m
[31m+             : zeroRefundByPolicy[39m
[31m+             ? "訂單已取消。依取消規則，本次退款金額為 NT$0。"[39m
[31m+             : "訂單已取消。",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // ── C：維持人工審核流程 ──[39m
[31m+       await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+         campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         event_type: "cancel_requested",[39m
[31m+         actor_label: order.guest_name,[39m
[31m+         note: reason ? `旅客申請取消訂單：${reason}` : "旅客申請取消訂單",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 通知：旅客取消申請確認信 + 民宿／管理者通知（寄信失敗不影響申請結果）[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const { data: accRow } = await admin[39m
[31m+           .from("accommodation_info")[39m
[31m+           .select("name")[39m
[31m+           .eq("id", (order as any).accommodation_id ?? campaign?.accommodation_id ?? "")[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+         const emailOrder = buildEmailOrder({[39m
[31m+           order,[39m
[31m+           campaign: campaign ?? { title: "團購活動", deposit_percentage: depositPct, payment_deadline_hours: 72 },[39m
[31m+           accommodationName: (accRow as any)?.name ?? null,[39m
[31m+           packageName: null,[39m
[31m+           frontendOrigin: typeof body.origin === "string" ? body.origin : null,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         const requestedAtText = formatTaipei(new Date());[39m
[31m+         const paidAmount = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).paid_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+         // 手續費一律讀館別設定（信用卡原卡退刷不收）[39m
[31m+         const isCardPay = String((order as any).payment_provider ?? "") === "tappay" &&[39m
[31m+           String((order as any).payment_method ?? "").includes("credit_card");[39m
[31m+         const handlingFee = isCardPay ? 0 : await loadManualRefundFee([39m
[31m+           admin,[39m
[31m+           (order as any).accommodation_id ?? campaign?.accommodation_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const guestMail = await sendGroupBuyEmail([39m
[31m+           order.guest_email,[39m
[31m+           renderGuestCancelRequested(emailOrder, { requestedAtText, reason: reason || null, handlingFee }),[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+           campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+           order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+           event_type: guestMail.ok ? "email_sent" : "email_failed",[39m
[31m+           actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+           note: `recipient_type:guest／template_type:guest_cancel_requested${[39m
[31m+             guestMail.ok ? "／recipient_count:1" : `／reason:${guestMail.reason ?? "unknown"}`[39m
[31m+           }`,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const { emails: venueEmails, source: venueSource } = await resolveOrderNotifyEmails(admin, {[39m
[31m+           accommodationId: (order as any).accommodation_id ?? campaign?.accommodation_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+           campaignId: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         if (venueEmails.length === 0) {[39m
[31m+           await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+             campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             event_type: "note",[39m
[31m+             actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+             note: "venue_email_skipped／recipient_type:venue／template_type:venue_cancel_requested／no_recipients",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         } else {[39m
[31m+           const venueMail = await sendGroupBuyEmail([39m
[31m+             venueEmails,[39m
[31m+             renderVenueCancelRequested([39m
[31m+               {[39m
[31m+                 ...emailOrder,[39m
[31m+                 guestPhone: order.guest_phone ?? null,[39m
[31m+                 guestEmail: order.guest_email ?? null,[39m
[31m+                 adminUrl: buildAdminGroupBuyUrl({[39m
[31m+                   accommodationId: (order as any).accommodation_id ?? campaign?.accommodation_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+                   campaignId: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+                   orderNumber: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+                 }),[39m
[31m+               },[39m
[31m+               { requestedAtText, reason: reason || null, paidAmount },[39m
[31m+             ),[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           await admin.from("group_buy_payment_events").insert({[39m
[31m+             campaign_id: campaign?.id ?? null,[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             event_type: venueMail.ok ? "email_sent" : "email_failed",[39m
[31m+             actor_label: "system",[39m
[31m+             note: `recipient_type:venue／template_type:venue_cancel_requested／recipient_count:${venueEmails.length}／source:${venueSource}${[39m
[31m+               venueMail.ok ? "" : `／reason:${venueMail.reason ?? "unknown"}`[39m
[31m+             }`,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch (notifyErr) {[39m
[31m+         console.error("[group-buy-order-lookup] cancel notify threw", notifyErr);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       return json({[39m
[31m+         success: true,[39m
[31m+         cancelRequested: true,[39m
[31m+         message: `${CANCEL_REQUEST_RECEIVED_TEXT}${REFUND_PENDING_REVIEW_TEXT}`,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // ── 民宿基本資料／訂房須知（正式規則）／入住前提醒／方案內容 ──[39m
[31m+     // 注意：訂房須知為 live data（顯示目前最新版本），非下單當時的條款快照。[39m
[31m+     const accommodationId = (order as any).accommodation_id ?? campaign?.accommodation_id ?? null;[39m
[31m+     const [{ data: acc }, { data: intro }, { data: bookingSettings }, { data: customSections }] = await Promise.all([[39m
[31m+       accommodationId[39m
[31m+         ? admin.from("accommodation_info").select("name, address, phone, check_in_notice, line_id, website_url, email").eq("id", accommodationId).maybeSingle()[39m
[31m+         : Promise.resolve({ data: null as any }),[39m
[31m+       accommodationId[39m
[31m+         ? admin[39m
[31m+           .from("group_buy_venue_intros")[39m
[31m+           .select("address, phone, check_in_notice")[39m
[31m+           .eq("accommodation_id", accommodationId)[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle()[39m
[31m+         : Promise.resolve({ data: null as any }),[39m
[31m+       accommodationId[39m
[31m+         ? admin.from("booking_settings").select("*").eq("accommodation_id", accommodationId).maybeSingle()[39m
[31m+         : Promise.resolve({ data: null as any }),[39m
[31m+       accommodationId[39m
[31m+         ? admin[39m
[31m+           .from("custom_sections")[39m
[31m+           .select("id, section_title, section_subtitle, section_content, display_order, is_enabled")[39m
[31m+           .eq("accommodation_id", accommodationId)[39m
[31m+           .order("display_order")[39m
[31m+         : Promise.resolve({ data: [] as any[] }),[39m
[31m+     ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // ── 常見問題：與團購活動頁完全同源 ──[39m
[31m+     // 資料表 faqs（accommodation_id + is_published，display_order 排序），[39m
[31m+     // 後台若於 group_buy_campaigns.faq_item_ids 指定，則依該陣列順序過濾。[39m
[31m+     let faqItems: { id: string; question: string; answer: string }[] = [];[39m
[31m+     if (accommodationId) {[39m
[31m+       const { data: faqRows } = await admin[39m
[31m+         .from("faqs")[39m
[31m+         .select("id, question, answer")[39m
[31m+         .eq("accommodation_id", accommodationId)[39m
[31m+         .eq("is_published", true)[39m
[31m+         .order("display_order");[39m
[31m+       const all = (faqRows ?? []) as { id: string; question: string; answer: string }[];[39m
[31m+       const picked = (campaign as any)?.faq_item_ids;[39m
[31m+       faqItems = Array.isArray(picked)[39m
[31m+         ? (picked.map((id: string) => all.find((f) => f.id === id)).filter(Boolean) as typeof all)[39m
[31m+         : all;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     let pkg: any = null;[39m
[31m+     if (campaign?.id) {[39m
[31m+       const { data: pkgs } = await admin[39m
[31m+         .from("group_buy_campaign_packages")[39m
[31m+         .select("display_name, description, image_url, group_buy_price, package_id, room_type_id, room_type_ids")[39m
[31m+         .eq("campaign_id", campaign.id);[39m
[31m+       const list = (pkgs ?? []) as any[];[39m
[31m+       // 建單時會把方案名稱寫進 notes（「方案：XXX × N 間 × M 晚」），優先用它精準比對，[39m
[31m+       // 避免同活動多方案共用房型時抓錯方案。[39m
[31m+       const noteName = String((order as any).notes ?? "").match(/方案：(.+?)\s*×/)?.[1]?.trim() ?? "";[39m
[31m+       pkg = (noteName ? list.find((p) => p.display_name === noteName) : null)[39m
[31m+         ?? list.find((p) => p.package_id && p.package_id === (order as any).package_id)[39m
[31m+         ?? list.find((p) =>[39m
[31m+           p.room_type_id === (order as any).room_type_id ||[39m
[31m+           (Array.isArray(p.room_type_ids) && p.room_type_ids.includes((order as any).room_type_id))[39m
[31m+         )[39m
[31m+         ?? (list.length === 1 ? list[0] : null);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const emailEvents = (events ?? []).filter((e: any) =>[39m
[31m+       e.event_type === "email_sent" || e.event_type === "email_failed"[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 團購信用卡＝全額付款：無訂金／尾款概念[39m
[31m+     const paidAmountNow = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).paid_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+     const fullPayment = String((order as any).payment_provider ?? "") === "tappay" &&[39m
[31m+       String((order as any).payment_method ?? "").includes("credit_card");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     /**[39m
[31m+      * 付款方式 SoT＝這張訂單的 orders.payment_method（不得依 campaign 現行設定推測）。[39m
[31m+      * atm → ATM 虛擬帳號；credit_card* → 信用卡；其餘 → 銀行人工匯款。[39m
[31m+      */[39m
[31m+     const paymentMethodRaw = String((order as any).payment_method ?? "");[39m
[31m+     const isAtmOrder = paymentMethodRaw === "atm" || !!(order as any).atm_vacc_no;[39m
[31m+     const paymentChannel: "atm" | "credit_card" | "bank" = fullPayment[39m
[31m+       ? "credit_card"[39m
[31m+       : isAtmOrder[39m
[31m+       ? "atm"[39m
[31m+       : "bank";[39m
[31m+     // 已收訂金／已付清：以 orders.payment_status 為準（ATM 不會有 admin_confirmed 事件）[39m
[31m+     const depositPaid = ["deposit_paid", "partial", "partial_paid", "paid"].includes([39m
[31m+       String((order as any).payment_status ?? ""),[39m
[31m+     ) || paidAmountNow > 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     /** 人工退款手續費（此館設定；信用卡原卡退刷一律 0）——前台文案唯一來源 */[39m
[31m+     const manualRefundFeeSetting = paymentChannel === "credit_card"[39m
[31m+       ? 0[39m
[31m+       : await loadManualRefundFee(admin, accommodationId);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return json({[39m
[31m+       order: {[39m
[31m+         orderNumber: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+         guestName: order.guest_name,[39m
[31m+         // 只回傳遮罩後的信箱給前台顯示（不外流完整 PII）[39m
[31m+         guestEmailMasked: (() => {[39m
[31m+           const em = String(order.guest_email ?? "");[39m
[31m+           const [local, domain] = em.split("@");[39m
[31m+           if (!domain || !local) return null;[39m
[31m+           return `${local.slice(0, 1)}${"*".repeat(Math.max(local.length - 1, 1))}@${domain}`;[39m
[31m+         })(),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         checkInDate: order.check_in_date,[39m
[31m+         checkOutDate: order.check_out_date,[39m
[31m+         guestCount: order.guest_count,[39m
[31m+         roomQuantity: order.room_quantity,[39m
[31m+         orderStatus: order.order_status,[39m
[31m+         paymentStatus: order.payment_status,[39m
[31m+         total,[39m
[31m+         deposit: fullPayment ? 0 : (Number(order.deposit_amount) > 0 ? Number(order.deposit_amount) : deposit),[39m
[31m+         balance: fullPayment ? Math.max(0, total - paidAmountNow) : balance,[39m
[31m+         depositPercentage: depositPct,[39m
[31m+         paymentProvider: (order as any).payment_provider ?? null,[39m
[31m+         paidAmount: paidAmountNow,[39m
[31m+         fullPayment,[39m
[31m+         /** 付款方式分流（前台顯示 SoT） */[39m
[31m+         paymentChannel,[39m
[31m+         paymentMethod: paymentMethodRaw || null,[39m
[31m+         depositPaid,[39m
[31m+         atm: isAtmOrder && (order as any).atm_vacc_no[39m
[31m+           ? {[39m
[31m+             vaccNo: (order as any).atm_vacc_no,[39m
[31m+             bankCode: (order as any).atm_vacc_bank_code ?? null,[39m
[31m+             expireAt: (order as any).atm_expire_at ?? null,[39m
[31m+             // 本地商業付款期限（新制）；NULL = legacy，前台沿用 provider 期限顯示[39m
[31m+             localDeadline: (order as any).group_buy_payment_deadline_at ?? null,[39m
[31m+             amount: Number(order.deposit_amount) > 0 ? Number(order.deposit_amount) : deposit,[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           : null,[39m
[31m+         // 取消政策依「此訂單」實際付款制度判定，不得依活動當下設定判定[39m
[31m+         cancellationPolicy: resolveCancellationPolicy({[39m
[31m+           order_source: (order as any).order_source ?? "group_buy",[39m
[31m+           payment_provider: (order as any).payment_provider ?? null,[39m
[31m+           payment_method: (order as any).payment_method ?? null,[39m
[31m+           paid_amount: paidAmountNow,[39m
[31m+           final_price: Number((order as any).final_price ?? (order as any).total_price ?? 0),[39m
[31m+         }).policy,[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         /** 人工退款手續費（此館設定；信用卡原卡退刷一律 0）——前台文案唯一來源 */[39m
[31m+         manualRefundFee: manualRefundFeeSetting,[39m
[31m+         refundFeeAmount: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).refund_fee_amount) || 0)),[39m
[31m+         refundedAmount: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).refunded_amount) || 0)),[39m
[31m+         remainingRefundable: remainingRefundableWithFee(order as any),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         paymentDeadline: deadline.toISOString(),[39m
[31m+         paymentDeadlineText: formatTaipei(deadline),[39m
[31m+       },[39m
[31m+       campaign: campaign[39m
[31m+         ? { title: campaign.title, slug: campaign.slug, contactNote: campaign.contact_note, kol: campaign.kol_display_name }[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+       bank: await (async () => {[39m
[31m+         // 只有「銀行人工匯款」訂單才回傳活動匯款帳戶；ATM／信用卡一律不得出現匯款帳號[39m
[31m+         if (paymentChannel !== "bank") return null;[39m
[31m+         if (!campaign?.id) return null;[39m
[31m+         const { data } = await admin[39m
[31m+           .from("group_buy_bank_accounts")[39m
[31m+           .select("*")[39m
[31m+           .eq("campaign_id", campaign.id)[39m
[31m+           .order("is_default", { ascending: false })[39m
[31m+           .order("created_at", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+           .limit(1)[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+         return bankToInfo(data);[39m
[31m+       })(),[39m
[31m+       events: events ?? [],[39m
[31m+       cancelRequest: cancelRequestedEvent[39m
[31m+         ? { requestedAt: (cancelRequestedEvent as any).created_at, note: (cancelRequestedEvent as any).note }[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 只回傳結構化欄位，raw note（內部 metadata）不外流給前台[39m
[31m+       emails: emailEvents.map((e: any) => {[39m
[31m+         const note = String(e.note ?? "");[39m
[31m+         const pick = (k: string) => note.match(new RegExp(`${k}:([^／/]+)`))?.[1]?.trim() ?? null;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           status: e.event_type === "email_sent" ? "sent" : "failed",[39m
[31m+           recipientType: pick("recipient_type"),[39m
[31m+           templateType: pick("template_type"),[39m
[31m+           createdAt: e.created_at,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       venue: acc[39m
[31m+         ? {[39m
[31m+           name: (acc as any).name ?? null,[39m
[31m+           address: (intro as any)?.address || (acc as any).address || null,[39m
[31m+           phone: (intro as any)?.phone || (acc as any).phone || null,[39m
[31m+           /** 人工退款申請引導用的客服管道（沿用館別 contact 設定，前台不得寫死） */[39m
[31m+           contact: {[39m
[31m+             phone: (intro as any)?.phone || (acc as any).phone || null,[39m
[31m+             lineId: (acc as any).line_id || null,[39m
[31m+             websiteUrl: (acc as any).website_url || null,[39m
[31m+             email: (acc as any).email || null,[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+       // 入住前提醒（訂單成立後才顯示的補充資訊），非正式住宿規則。[39m
[31m+       // 有快照時優先使用快照版本（保留下單當時提供給客人的內容）。[39m
[31m+       preArrivalNotice: (order as any).group_buy_booking_notice_snapshot?.post_booking_notice[39m
[31m+         ?? (intro as any)?.check_in_notice ?? null,[39m
[31m+       // 常見問題：與團購活動頁完全同源（faqs 表 + campaign.faq_item_ids 指定順序）[39m
[31m+       faqItems: faqItems,[39m
[31m+       // 註：訂房須知快照（orders.group_buy_booking_notice_snapshot）仍完整保留於資料庫，[39m
[31m+       // 供後台稽核／爭議舉證使用，僅不在旅客訂單查詢頁顯示。[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       package: pkg[39m
[31m+         ? {[39m
[31m+           name: pkg.display_name,[39m
[31m+           description: pkg.description,[39m
[31m+           imageUrl: pkg.image_url,[39m
[31m+           price: Number(pkg.group_buy_price) || 0,[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         : null,[39m
[31m+       refund: (() => {[39m
[31m+         const o = order as any;[39m
[31m+         const paid = paidAmountNow;[39m
[31m+         const refundedAmount = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(o.refunded_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+         const feeAmount = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(o.refund_fee_amount) || 0));[39m
[31m+         const settled = refundedAmount + feeAmount;[39m
[31m+         const isCard = paymentChannel === "credit_card";[39m
[31m+         /**[39m
[31m+          * 「政策應退」一律沿用 server 既有 quoteCancellation（不另建一套算法）：[39m
[31m+          *  - 已有沖銷紀錄 → 直接用實際沖銷總額（實退 + 手續費），與帳務一致。[39m
[31m+          *  - 尚未退款 → 以取消日（cancelled_at 的台北日期，無則今日）試算。[39m
[31m+          */[39m
[31m+         const cancelDate = o.cancelled_at[39m
[31m+           ? new Date(o.cancelled_at).toLocaleDateString("en-CA", { timeZone: "Asia/Taipei" })[39m
[31m+           : taipeiToday();[39m
[31m+         const quote = quoteCancellation({[39m
[31m+           order: {[39m
[31m+             order_source: o.order_source ?? "group_buy",[39m
[31m+             payment_provider: o.payment_provider ?? null,[39m
[31m+             payment_method: o.payment_method ?? null,[39m
[31m+             deposit_payment_method: o.deposit_payment_method ?? null,[39m
[31m+             balance_payment_method: o.balance_payment_method ?? null,[39m
[31m+             paid_amount: paid,[39m
[31m+             final_price: Number(o.final_price ?? o.total_price ?? 0),[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+           checkInDate: String(o.check_in_date),[39m
[31m+           cancellationDate: cancelDate,[39m
[31m+           alreadyRefunded: 0,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         const policyRefundAmount = settled > 0[39m
[31m+           ? settled[39m
[31m+           : Math.min(paid, Math.max(0, Math.round(quote.refundAmount)));[39m
[31m+         const feeApplies = !isCard && policyRefundAmount > 0;[39m
[31m+         const expectedFee = feeAmount > 0[39m
[31m+           ? feeAmount[39m
[31m+           : feeApplies[39m
[31m+           ? Math.min(manualRefundFeeSetting, policyRefundAmount)[39m
[31m+           : 0;[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           status: o.refund_status ?? null,[39m
[31m+           amount: refundedAmount,[39m
[31m+           refundedAt: o.refunded_at ?? null,[39m
[31m+           paidAmount: paid,[39m
[31m+           policyRefundAmount,[39m
[31m+           feeAmount,[39m
[31m+           /** 尚未退款時的預估手續費（已退款則等於實際 feeAmount） */[39m
[31m+           expectedFeeAmount: expectedFee,[39m
[31m+           expectedPayoutAmount: Math.max(0, policyRefundAmount - expectedFee),[39m
[31m+           refundedAmount,[39m
[31m+           remainingRefundable: Math.max(0, paid - settled),[39m
[31m+           paymentMethodKind: paymentMethodKind(o),[39m
[31m+           paymentMethodLabel: paymentMethodLabel(o),[39m
[31m+           refundChannelLabel: refundChannelLabel(isCard),[39m
[31m+           isCardOrder: isCard,[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       })(),[39m
[31m+       /**[39m
[31m+        * 旅客自助取消分流（前台文案用）：[39m
[31m+        * direct_cancel / card_auto_refund / technical_processing / manual_review[39m
[31m+        * 信用卡永遠不會回 manual_review（不存在人工取消審核）。[39m
[31m+        */[39m
[31m+       selfCancel: (() => {[39m
[31m+         const cls = classifyGroupBuySelfCancel(order as any);[39m
[31m+         if (cls.isCardOrder) {[39m
[31m+           const q = quoteCancellation({[39m
[31m+             order: order as any,[39m
[31m+             checkInDate: String(order.check_in_date),[39m
[31m+             cancellationDate: taipeiToday(),[39m
[31m+             alreadyRefunded: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number((order as any).refunded_amount) || 0)),[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           const exception = detectCardSelfCancelException({[39m
[31m+             checkInDate: order.check_in_date,[39m
[31m+             paidAmount: (order as any).paid_amount,[39m
[31m+             policyRequiresManualReview: q.requiresManualReview,[39m
[31m+             policyRefundAmount: q.refundAmount,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (exception) return { mode: "technical_processing", estimatedRefund: 0 };[39m
[31m+           const r = resolveSelfCancelRefundAmount({[39m
[31m+             refundable: cls.refundable,[39m
[31m+             policyRefundAmount: q.refundAmount,[39m
[31m+             requiresManualReview: false,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           return { mode: "card_auto_refund", estimatedRefund: r.refundAmount };[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return { mode: cls.mode, estimatedRefund: 0 };[39m
[31m+       })(),[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+     console.error(`[${FN}] error`, e);[39m
[31m+     // 對客一律回傳友善文案，原始 exception 只留在 server log[39m
[31m+     return json({ error: "目前無法完成操作，請稍後再試或聯繫客服。" }, 500);[39m
[31m+   }[39m

[31m+ interface OrderDetailPageProps {[39m
[31m+   orderId?: string;[39m
[31m+   embedded?: boolean;[39m
[31m+   onClose?: () => void;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ const PAYMENT_DAY_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 訂金（建立日 +3 天）與尾款（入住日 +3 天，可提前付清）期限判斷，供徽章與說明區共用 */[39m
[31m+ function getPaymentDeadlineInfo(order: any, isNonPayableCurrency: boolean) {[39m
[31m+   const base = order?.payment_link_created_at || order?.created_at;[39m
[31m+   const depositDue = base ? new Date(new Date(base).getTime() + 3 * PAYMENT_DAY_MS) : null;[39m
[31m+   const balanceDue = order?.check_in_date[39m
[31m+     ? new Date(new Date(`${order.check_in_date}T00:00:00+08:00`).getTime() + 3 * PAYMENT_DAY_MS)[39m
[31m+     : null;[39m
[31m+   const now = Date.now();[39m
[31m+   const status = order?.payment_status;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   let effectiveStatus = status;[39m
[31m+   let stage: "deposit" | "balance" | "none" = "none";[39m
[31m+   let reason = "";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (isNonPayableCurrency) {[39m
[31m+     reason = "此訂單使用非線上金流幣別，不套用逾期判斷。";[39m
[31m+   } else if (["unpaid", "partial"].includes(status)) {[39m
[31m+     stage = "deposit";[39m
[31m+     if (depositDue && now > depositDue.getTime()) {[39m
[31m+       effectiveStatus = "overdue";[39m
[31m+       reason = "已超過訂金繳款期限（建立訂單起 3 天）且尚未收到訂金，故顯示已逾期。";[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       reason = "尚在訂金繳款期限內（建立訂單起 3 天），故維持原付款狀態。";[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } else if (status === "deposit_paid") {[39m
[31m+     stage = "balance";[39m
[31m+     if (balanceDue && now > balanceDue.getTime()) {[39m
[31m+       effectiveStatus = "overdue";[39m
[31m+       reason = "訂金已收，但已超過尾款繳款期限（入住日後 3 天），故顯示已逾期。";[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m
[31m+       reason = "訂金已收，尾款期限為入住日後 3 天（可提前付清），尚未到期，故顯示已付訂金。";[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   } else if (status === "paid") {[39m
[31m+     reason = "款項已全額付清，不再判斷期限。";[39m
[31m+   } else {[39m
[31m+     reason = "此付款狀態不套用期限判斷。";[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return { effectiveStatus, stage, reason, depositDue, balanceDue };[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ // savedAddonTotalOf / pureRoomFeeOf 已抽到 @/lib/orderAddonTotal（與 DB calc_order_addon_total 同口徑）[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+   const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);[39m

[31m+   const [editExclusiveConfigs, setEditExclusiveConfigs] = useState<Array<Record<string, number>>>([]);[39m
[31m+   const setExclusiveConfigAt = useCallback((idx: number, name: string, value: number) => {[39m
[31m+     setEditExclusiveConfigs(prev => {[39m
[31m+       const next = [...prev];[39m
[31m+       while (next.length <= idx) next.push({});[39m
[31m+       next[idx] = { ...next[idx], [name]: value };[39m
[31m+       return next;[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+   // 只有森山寫照保留「包區」用語，其餘民宿一律使用「包棟」[39m
[31m+   const SENSAN_ACC_ID = "5f764d55-ae11-4631-80fe-2e611853deae";[39m
[31m+   const HAKUNA_ACC_ID = "6b6ac0dc-602b-4dec-9167-7791ac412ba3";[39m
[31m+   const YUNXIU_ACC_ID = "7690a606-6bac-4cd5-a3e7-2fa754b318e8";[39m
[31m+   const OLIVIA_ACC_ID = "74d064f4-1f8c-47e8-9be0-f0a596bbe6f8";[39m
[31m+   const isSensan = order?.accommodation_id === SENSAN_ACC_ID;[39m
[31m+   const isHakuna = order?.accommodation_id === HAKUNA_ACC_ID;[39m
[31m+   const isYunxiu = order?.accommodation_id === YUNXIU_ACC_ID;[39m
[31m+   const isOlivia = order?.accommodation_id === OLIVIA_ACC_ID;[39m
[31m+   const { config: secondaryCurrencyConfig } = useSecondaryCurrency(order?.accommodation_id);[39m
[31m+   // 每筆訂單獨立的顯示幣別[39m
[31m+   const [orderDisplayCurrency, setOrderDisplayCurrency] = useState<string | null>(null);[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order?.id) return;[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       const { data } = await supabase.from("order_meta").select("display_currency").eq("order_id", order.id).maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+       if (!cancelled) setOrderDisplayCurrency((data as any)?.display_currency ?? null);[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+     return () => { cancelled = true; };[39m
[31m+   }, [order?.id]);[39m
[31m+   const currencyForcedMode = useMemo<"TWD" | "SEC" | null>(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!secondaryCurrencyConfig) return null;[39m
[31m+     if (!orderDisplayCurrency) return null;[39m
[31m+     return orderDisplayCurrency.toUpperCase() === secondaryCurrencyConfig.code.toUpperCase() ? "SEC" : "TWD";[39m
[31m+   }, [orderDisplayCurrency, secondaryCurrencyConfig]);[39m
[31m+   const editFormAmountsRef = useRef<{ total_price: number; discount_amount: number; addon_total: number; paid_amount: number; isEditing: boolean }>({ total_price: 0, discount_amount: 0, addon_total: 0, paid_amount: 0, isEditing: false });[39m
[31m+   const clearEditAmountsRef = useRef<() => void>(() => {});[39m
[31m+   const handleCurrencyModeChange = useCallback(async (m: "TWD" | "SEC") => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order?.id) return;[39m
[31m+     const code = (await import("@/lib/orderDisplayCurrency")).modeToCurrencyCode(m, secondaryCurrencyConfig?.code);[39m
[31m+     const currentUpper = (orderDisplayCurrency || "TWD").toUpperCase();[39m
[31m+     if (currentUpper === code.toUpperCase()) return;[39m
[31m+     const { total_price, discount_amount, addon_total, paid_amount, isEditing: editing } = editFormAmountsRef.current;[39m
[31m+     const hasAmounts = editing && [total_price, discount_amount, addon_total, paid_amount].some(v => Number(v) > 0);[39m
[31m+     if (hasAmounts) {[39m
[31m+       const ok = window.confirm(`切換幣別會清空目前的金額 / 折扣 / 加購 / 已收金額（避免 NT$ 與 ${secondaryCurrencyConfig?.symbol || "外幣"} 混算錯誤）。確定切換嗎？`);[39m
[31m+       if (!ok) return;[39m
[31m+       clearEditAmountsRef.current();[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setOrderDisplayCurrency(code);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       await (await import("@/lib/orderDisplayCurrency")).setOrderDisplayCurrency(order.id, code);[39m
[31m+     } catch (e: any) {[39m
[31m+       console.error("[displayCurrency] failed", e);[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+         title: "幣別切換失敗",[39m
[31m+         description: e?.message || "幣別標記未能寫入，請稍後再試。",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [order?.id, secondaryCurrencyConfig?.code, secondaryCurrencyConfig?.symbol, orderDisplayCurrency, toast]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // 訂單顯示貨幣前綴（旅行日 JPY 時原幣顯示，其他 TWD）[39m
[31m+   const orderCurrencyPrefix = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!secondaryCurrencyConfig) return "NT$";[39m
[31m+     const cur = (orderDisplayCurrency || "TWD").toUpperCase();[39m
[31m+     return cur === secondaryCurrencyConfig.code.toUpperCase() ? secondaryCurrencyConfig.symbol : "NT$";[39m
[31m+   }, [orderDisplayCurrency, secondaryCurrencyConfig]);[39m
[31m+   const isRawCurrency = !!secondaryCurrencyConfig;[39m
[31m+   // 旅行日 JPY 訂單：純內部記帳，不走任何第三方金流[39m

[31m+   const exclusiveLabel = isSensan ? "包區" : "包棟";[39m

[31m+   const fallbackBreakdownItems = useMemo<Array<{ name: string; max: number }>>(() => {[39m

[31m+   const [defaultDepositRatio, setDefaultDepositRatio] = useState<number>(0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Load accommodation booking settings (use_deposit / deposit_percentage) for fallback ratio[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m

[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       const { data } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("booking_settings")[39m
[31m+         .select("use_deposit, deposit_percentage")[39m
[31m+         .eq("accommodation_id", order.accommodation_id)[39m
[31m+         .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+       if (data && data.use_deposit !== false) {[39m
[31m+         setDefaultDepositRatio((data.deposit_percentage ?? 30) / 100);[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         setDefaultDepositRatio(0);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+   }, [order?.accommodation_id]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getRoomUnitPrice = useCallback((roomPrice: number, quantity: number) => {[39m
[31m+     return quantity > 0 ? Math.round(roomPrice / quantity) : roomPrice;[39m
[31m+   }, []);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getSuggestedRoomUnitPrice = useCallback((roomTypeId: string, currentRoomId?: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const candidatePrices = [[39m
[31m+       ...editOrderRooms[39m
[31m+         .filter((room) => room.room_type_id === roomTypeId && room.id !== currentRoomId)[39m
[31m+         .map((room) => room.unit_price || getRoomUnitPrice(room.room_price, room.quantity)),[39m
[31m+       ...orderRooms[39m
[31m+         .filter((room) => room.room_type_id === roomTypeId && room.id !== currentRoomId)[39m
[31m+         .map((room) => getRoomUnitPrice(room.room_price, room.quantity)),[39m
[31m+     ].filter((price) => price > 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const roomTypeBasePrice = roomTypes.find((roomType) => roomType.id === roomTypeId)?.base_price ?? 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (roomTypeBasePrice > 0) {[39m
[31m+       candidatePrices.push(roomTypeBasePrice);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return candidatePrices.length > 0 ? Math.min(...candidatePrices) : 0;[39m
[31m+   }, [editOrderRooms, getRoomUnitPrice, orderRooms, roomTypes]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * 依「訂單入住日期」與該房型的對應方案 (template) 透過 templatePriceResolver[39m
[31m+    * 計算每晚價格總和，回傳「單間總額（含所有晚數）」。[39m
[31m+    * 若找不到方案或日期區間，回傳 0（呼叫端會 fallback 到舊邏輯）。[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   const computeRoomTotalByDates = useCallback(async ([39m
[31m+     roomTypeId: string,[39m
[31m+     templateName: string | null | undefined,[39m
[31m+   ): Promise<number> => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order?.check_in_date || !order?.check_out_date || !roomTypeId) return 0;[39m
[31m+     const start = new Date(order.check_in_date);[39m
[31m+     const end = new Date(order.check_out_date);[39m
[31m+     if (!(start < end)) return 0;[39m
[31m+     const dates = eachDayOfInterval({ start, end: addDays(end, -1) }).map(d => format(d, "yyyy-MM-dd"));[39m
[31m+     if (!dates.length) return 0;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 候選方案：包含「未綁定房型 (room_type_id = null，視為全房型通用，如包棟方案)」與「綁定本房型」的 templates[39m
[31m+     const candidates = allTemplates.filter(t => templateMatchesRoomType(t as any, roomTypeId));[39m
[31m+     let template: PricingTemplateLite | null = null;[39m
[31m+     if (templateName) {[39m
[31m+       // 1) 精準同名[39m
[31m+       template = candidates.find(t => t.template_name === templateName) || null;[39m
[31m+       // 2) 同 meal plan 類別（二食 / 三食 / 一食）[39m
[31m+       if (!template) {[39m
[31m+         const mealTag = templateName.includes("三食") || templateName.includes("3食")[39m
[31m+           ? "三食"[39m
[31m+           : templateName.includes("二食") || templateName.includes("2食") || templateName.includes("兩食")[39m
[31m+             ? "二食"[39m
[31m+             : null;[39m
[31m+         if (mealTag) {[39m
[31m+           template = candidates.find(t => (t.template_name || "").includes(mealTag)) || null;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // 3) 退而求其次：該房型的第一個 template[39m
[31m+     if (!template) {[39m
[31m+       template = candidates[0] || null;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const priceMap = await resolveTemplatePricesForDates({[39m
[31m+       roomTypeId,[39m
[31m+       template,[39m
[31m+       dates,[39m
[31m+       publicHolidays,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     let total = 0;[39m
[31m+     for (const d of dates) {[39m
[31m+       if (priceMap[d] != null) total += priceMap[d];[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return Math.round(total);[39m
[31m+   }, [order?.check_in_date, order?.check_out_date, allTemplates, publicHolidays]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState<EditFormState>({[39m
[31m+     guest_name: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_phone: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_email: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_address: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_gender: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_nationality: "",[39m
[31m+     guest_count: 1,[39m
[31m+     order_source: "",[39m
[31m+     owner_direct: false,[39m
[31m+     lock_whole_building: false,[39m
[31m+     order_status: "",[39m
[31m+     payment_status: "",[39m
[31m+     deposit_payment_method: "",[39m
[31m+     balance_payment_method: "",[39m
[31m+     meal_plan: "",[39m
[31m+     notes: "",[39m
[31m+     room_type_id: "",[39m
[31m+     total_price: 0,[39m
[31m+     discount_amount: 0,[39m
[31m+     discount_code: "",[39m
[31m+     deposit_amount: 0,[39m
[31m+     paid_amount: 0,[39m
[31m+     pet_count: 0,[39m
[31m+     pet_plan_price: 0,[39m
[31m+     meal_plan_details: [],[39m
[31m+     meal_plan_total: 0,[39m
[31m+     custom_addon_details: [],[39m
[31m+     addon_total: 0,[39m
[31m+     addon_note: "",[39m
[31m+     extra_addon_amount: 0,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Load 包棟方案的內含房間清單（room_types.package_contents）[39m
[31m+   // 每一筆編輯中的包棟房間各自查自己的房型；另含訂單原本／編輯中的 room_type_id（單房型畫面用）。[39m
[31m+   const breakdownRoomTypeIds = useMemo(() => {[39m
[31m+     const ids = editOrderRooms[39m
[31m+       .filter(r => roomTypes.find(t => t.id === r.room_type_id)?.is_exclusive_package)[39m

[31m+     if (order?.room_type_id) ids.push(order.room_type_id);[39m

[31m+     return ids;[39m
[31m+   }, [editOrderRooms, roomTypes, order?.room_type_id, editForm.room_type_id]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const { getBreakdown } = usePackageBreakdown(breakdownRoomTypeIds);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /** 取得「某一筆房間」該顯示的組成欄位（有 DB 設定用 DB，否則 fallback 舊三家 hardcoded） */[39m
[31m+   const getBreakdownItemsFor = useCallback([39m
[31m+     (roomTypeId?: string | null): Array<{ name: string; max: number }> => {[39m
[31m+       const items = getBreakdown(roomTypeId);[39m
[31m+       if (items && items.length > 0) return items.map(b => ({ name: b.name, max: b.count }));[39m
[31m+       return fallbackBreakdownItems;[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     [getBreakdown, fallbackBreakdownItems],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   /** 訂單標題區（單一房型畫面）的組成欄位：依編輯中的 room_type_id */[39m
[31m+   const headerBreakdownItems = useMemo([39m
[31m+     () => getBreakdownItemsFor(editForm.room_type_id),[39m
[31m+     [getBreakdownItemsFor, editForm.room_type_id],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * 「換成新包棟房型」當下帶入該房型 package_contents 的預設間數。[39m
[31m+    * 只在切換房型這一次動作呼叫；初次開啟訂單沿用備註既有數值，之後渲染也不會覆蓋手動值。[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   const applyExclusiveDefaultsOnRoomTypeChange = useCallback([39m
[31m+     async (roomsAfterChange: Array<{ id: string; room_type_id: string }>, rowId: string, newRoomTypeId: string) => {[39m
[31m+       const rt = roomTypes.find(t => t.id === newRoomTypeId);[39m
[31m+       if (!rt?.is_exclusive_package) return;[39m
[31m+       const exclusiveIds = roomsAfterChange[39m
[31m+         .filter(r => roomTypes.find(t => t.id === r.room_type_id)?.is_exclusive_package)[39m
[31m+         .map(r => r.id);[39m
[31m+       const idx = exclusiveIds.indexOf(rowId);[39m
[31m+       if (idx < 0) return;[39m
[31m+       let items = getBreakdown(newRoomTypeId);[39m
[31m+       if (!items) {[39m
[31m+         const { data } = await supabase[39m
[31m+           .from("room_types")[39m
[31m+           .select("package_contents")[39m
[31m+           .eq("id", newRoomTypeId)[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+         const normalized = normalizePackageContents((data as any)?.package_contents);[39m
[31m+         items = normalized.length > 0 ? normalized : null;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       const defaults = items ? defaultCountsByNameFromBreakdown(items) : {};[39m
[31m+       setEditExclusiveConfigs(prev => {[39m
[31m+         const next = [...prev];[39m
[31m+         while (next.length <= idx) next.push({});[39m
[31m+         next[idx] = defaults;[39m
[31m+         return next;[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     },[39m
[31m+     [roomTypes, getBreakdown],[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+   // Sync amount refs for the currency-toggle guard defined above[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     editFormAmountsRef.current = {[39m
[31m+       total_price: Number(editForm.total_price) || 0,[39m
[31m+       discount_amount: Number(editForm.discount_amount) || 0,[39m
[31m+       addon_total: Number(editForm.addon_total) || 0,[39m
[31m+       paid_amount: Number(editForm.paid_amount) || 0,[39m
[31m+       isEditing,[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     clearEditAmountsRef.current = () => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, total_price: 0, discount_amount: 0, addon_total: 0, paid_amount: 0 }));[39m
[31m+   }, [editForm.total_price, editForm.discount_amount, editForm.addon_total, editForm.paid_amount, isEditing]);[39m
[31m+   const [discountCodeError, setDiscountCodeError] = useState("");[39m
[31m+   // 已結清訂單的折扣一律改由「修改金額」對話框處理（權限：/admin 同館訂單模組 editable），[39m
[31m+   // inline 編輯不再提供已付款折扣修改入口，避免與房型重建流程耦合。[39m
[31m+   const INLINE_PAID_DISCOUNT_EDITABLE = false;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const startEditing = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     // readonly / 無權限：不得進入編輯模式（房間新增、移除、rebuild 皆在編輯模式內）[39m
[31m+     if (!canManageOrders) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "權限不足",[39m
[31m+         description: "您目前對「訂單」模組僅有檢視權限，無法編輯訂單。",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // Parse exclusive package room configs from notes (one line per 包棟)[39m
[31m+     const notesStr = order.notes || "";[39m
[31m+     // 逐行擷取「【包區/包棟房間配置】...」，動態解析各房型數量（兼容舊「雙人房/四人房」格式）[39m
[31m+     const pkgLineRegex = /【\s*(?:包區|包棟)\s*房間配置\s*】\s*(.+)/;[39m
[31m+     const parsedConfigs: Array<Record<string, number>> = [];[39m
[31m+     notesStr.split(/\r?\n/).forEach(line => {[39m
[31m+       const lm = line.match(pkgLineRegex);[39m
[31m+       if (!lm) return;[39m
[31m+       const body = lm[1];[39m
[31m+       // 解析格式：「房型名稱 N 間」逐段[39m
[31m+       const cfg: Record<string, number> = {};[39m
[31m+       const partRegex = /([\u4e00-\u9fff\w]+?)\s*(\d+)\s*間/g;[39m
[31m+       let m: RegExpExecArray | null;[39m
[31m+       while ((m = partRegex.exec(body)) !== null) {[39m
[31m+         const name = m[1].trim();[39m
[31m+         const n = parseInt(m[2]) || 0;[39m
[31m+         if (name) cfg[name] = n;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (Object.keys(cfg).length > 0) parsedConfigs.push(cfg);[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     setEditExclusiveConfigs(parsedConfigs);[39m
[31m+     // Snapshot 編輯前的房間配置與包區設定，供儲存時計算差異[39m
[31m+     initialEditSnapshot.current = {[39m
[31m+       rooms: (orderRooms || []).map(r => ({[39m
[31m+         room_type_id: r.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+         quantity: r.quantity,[39m
[31m+         room_price: r.room_price,[39m
[31m+         room_unit_id: r.room_unit_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+       })),[39m
[31m+       exclusiveConfigs: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(parsedConfigs)),[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const savedCustomAddons = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+     const savedPetFromCustom = savedCustomAddons[39m
[31m+       .filter(d => d.addon_type === 'pet')[39m
[31m+       .reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0);[39m
[31m+     const savedMealFromCustom = savedCustomAddons[39m
[31m+       .filter(d => d.addon_type === 'meal')[39m
[31m+       .reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0);[39m
[31m+     const savedOtherCustom = savedCustomAddons[39m
[31m+       .filter(d => d.addon_type !== 'pet' && d.addon_type !== 'meal' && d.addon_type !== 'bbq')[39m
[31m+       .reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0);[39m
[31m+     const savedExtraMatch = (order.notes || "").match(/【額外現場加購金額[^】]*】\n?(\d+)\n?【額外現場加購金額END】/);[39m
[31m+     const savedExtraAddon = savedExtraMatch ? parseInt(savedExtraMatch[1], 10) || 0 : 0;[39m
[31m+     const savedAddonTotal =[39m
[31m+       ((order.pet_plan_price || 0) > 0 ? order.pet_plan_price : savedPetFromCustom) +[39m
[31m+       ((order.meal_plan_total || 0) > 0 ? order.meal_plan_total : savedMealFromCustom) +[39m
[31m+       savedOtherCustom +[39m
[31m+       savedExtraAddon;[39m
[31m+     // 新訂單的 total_price 已含加購；舊訂單則可能未含。以已存 final_price 判斷，[39m

[31m+     const totalAlreadyIncludesAddons = Math.abs([39m
[31m+       Number(order.final_price || 0) - (Number(order.total_price || 0) - Number(order.discount_amount || 0)),[39m
[31m+     ) <= 1;[39m
[31m+     const editableRoomPrice = Math.max([39m
[31m+       0,[39m
[31m+       Number(order.total_price || 0) - (totalAlreadyIncludesAddons ? savedAddonTotal : 0),[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     setEditForm({[39m
[31m+       guest_name: order.guest_name || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_phone: order.guest_phone || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_email: order.guest_email || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_address: order.guest_address || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_gender: order.guest_gender || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_nationality: order.guest_nationality || "",[39m
[31m+       guest_count: order.guest_count || 1,[39m
[31m+       order_source: order.order_source || "website",[39m
[31m+       owner_direct: !!order.owner_direct,[39m
[31m+       lock_whole_building: !!order.lock_whole_building,[39m
[31m+       order_status: order.order_status || "pending",[39m
[31m+       payment_status: order.payment_status || "unpaid",[39m
[31m+       deposit_payment_method: order.deposit_payment_method || "none",[39m
[31m+       balance_payment_method: order.balance_payment_method || "none",[39m
[31m+       meal_plan: (() => {[39m
[31m+         const raw = order.meal_plan;[39m
[31m+         if (!raw) return "none";[39m
[31m+         // Legacy: meal_plan stored as template UUID — normalize to template_name[39m
[31m+         const byId = roomTemplates.find(t => t.id === raw) || allRoomTemplates.find(t => t.id === raw);[39m
[31m+         if (byId) return byId.template_name;[39m

[31m+       // 同時把改名前的舊快照（例如「加床服務」）對應回現行設定並合併數量，避免出現兩筆重複加購。[39m
[31m+       custom_addon_details: (() => {[39m
[31m+         const raw = ((order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [])[39m

[31m+             ? { ...d, addon_id: matched.id, addon_type: matched.addon_type }[39m
[31m+             : d;[39m
[31m+           const hit = merged.find(m =>[39m
[31m+             (canonical.addon_id && m.addon_id === canonical.addon_id) || m.addon_type === canonical.addon_type,[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           if (hit) {[39m
[31m+             hit.count += canonical.count;[39m
[31m+             hit.total += canonical.total;[39m
[31m+           } else {[39m
[31m+             merged.push({ ...canonical });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return merged;[39m
[31m+       })(),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       addon_total: savedAddonTotal,[39m
[31m+       addon_note: extractAddonNote(order.notes),[39m
[31m+       extra_addon_amount: extractExtraAddonAmount(order.notes),[39m

[31m+     // Initialize edit order rooms[39m

[31m+       const normalizedRooms = orderRooms.map((or) => {[39m
[31m+         const correctedRoomPrice = shouldUseOrderTotalForSingleRoom ? editableRoomPrice : Number(or.room_price || 0);[39m
[31m+         const unitPrice = getRoomUnitPrice(correctedRoomPrice, or.quantity);[39m

[31m+         ? editableRoomPrice[39m
[31m+         : normalizedRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+       setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, total_price: roomsTotal }));[39m
[31m+     } else {[39m

[31m+       if (order.room_type_id && orderRoomsLoaded) {[39m
