[31m+           id: `synthetic-${order.id}`,[39m
[31m+           room_type_id: order.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+           quantity: 1,[39m
[31m+           room_price: syntheticPrice,[39m
[31m+           unit_price: syntheticPrice,[39m
[31m+           template_id: null,[39m
[31m+           template_name: null,[39m
[31m+           room_unit_id: null,[39m
[31m+         }]);[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         setEditOrderRooms([]);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // Store deposit ratio based on original final price[39m
[31m+     const savedRoomsTotalForRatio = orderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+     const effectiveTotal = orderRooms.length === 1 && editableRoomPrice > 0 && savedRoomsTotalForRatio !== editableRoomPrice[39m
[31m+       ? editableRoomPrice[39m
[31m+       : (orderRooms.length > 0 ? savedRoomsTotalForRatio : editableRoomPrice);[39m
[31m+     // 訂金比例僅以「房費 - 折扣」為基準，加購方案不影響訂金[39m
[31m+     const originalBase = Math.max(0, effectiveTotal - (order.discount_amount || 0));[39m
[31m+     const existingRatio = originalBase > 0 ? (order.deposit_amount || 0) / originalBase : 0;[39m
[31m+     // Fallback to accommodation default deposit ratio when order has no deposit yet[39m
[31m+     depositRatioRef.current = existingRatio > 0 ? existingRatio : defaultDepositRatio;[39m
[31m+     originalDiscountCode.current = order.discount_code || "";[39m
[31m+     openedUpdatedAtRef.current = (order as any).updated_at ?? null;[39m
[31m+     syntheticDecisionRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     setIsEditing(true);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-sync addon_total when individual addons change[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing) return;[39m
[31m+     const computed = editForm.pet_plan_price + editForm.meal_plan_total + editForm.custom_addon_details.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0) + (editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0);[39m
[31m+     if (computed !== editForm.addon_total) {[39m
[31m+       setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, addon_total: computed }));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, editForm.pet_plan_price, editForm.meal_plan_total, editForm.custom_addon_details, editForm.extra_addon_amount]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-sync deposit_amount when final price changes[39m
[31m+   // Skip when order is already paid/deposit_paid — deposit must stay locked[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing || depositRatioRef.current <= 0) return;[39m
[31m+     if (order && ["deposit_paid", "paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status)) return;[39m
[31m+     const newBase = Math.max(0, editForm.total_price - editForm.discount_amount);[39m
[31m+     const newDeposit = Math.max(0, Math.round(newBase * depositRatioRef.current));[39m
[31m+     if (newDeposit !== editForm.deposit_amount) {[39m
[31m+       setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, deposit_amount: newDeposit }));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, editForm.total_price, editForm.discount_amount, order?.payment_status]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-lookup discount code and apply discount amount[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing || !order) return;[39m
[31m+     const code = editForm.discount_code.trim();[39m
[31m+     const originalCode = (originalDiscountCode.current || "").trim();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     if (!code) {[39m
[31m+       setDiscountCodeError("");[39m
[31m+       // Only clear discount if the user explicitly removed an existing code.[39m
[31m+       // If the order never had a code (manual discount), preserve the amount.[39m
[31m+       if (originalCode) {[39m
[31m+         setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, discount_amount: 0 }));[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // If the discount code hasn't changed from the original, preserve the saved discount_amount[39m
[31m+     if (code === originalCode) {[39m
[31m+       setDiscountCodeError("");[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const timer = setTimeout(async () => {[39m
[31m+       setDiscountCodeError("");[39m
[31m+       const base = editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total;[39m
[31m+       // Use the primary room_type_id for validation[39m
[31m+       const roomTypeId = editForm.room_type_id || (editOrderRooms.length > 0 ? editOrderRooms[0].room_type_id : null) || order.room_type_id || null;[39m
[31m+       const checkInDate = order.check_in_date || null;[39m
[31m+       const pricingTemplateId = await getOrderPricingTemplateId(order.id);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const { data, error } = await supabase.rpc('validate_discount_code', {[39m
[31m+           p_code: code.toUpperCase(),[39m
[31m+           p_accommodation_id: order.accommodation_id,[39m
[31m+           p_order_amount: base,[39m
[31m+           p_room_type_id: roomTypeId,[39m
[31m+           p_check_in_date: checkInDate,[39m
[31m+           p_pricing_template_id: pricingTemplateId[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (error) {[39m
[31m+           setDiscountCodeError("驗證折扣碼失敗");[39m
[31m+           return;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const result = data?.[0];[39m
[31m+         if (!result || !result.is_valid) {[39m
[31m+           let errorMsg = result?.message || "此折扣碼不存在";[39m
[31m+           if (result?.applicable_room_names && result.applicable_room_names.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+             errorMsg += `（僅適用：${result.applicable_room_names.join('、')}）`;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           setDiscountCodeError(errorMsg);[39m
[31m+           setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, discount_amount: 0 }));[39m
[31m+           return;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         let newDiscount = 0;[39m
[31m+         if (result.discount_type === "percentage") {[39m
[31m+           newDiscount = Math.round(base * result.discount_value / 100);[39m
[31m+         } else {[39m
[31m+           newDiscount = Math.min(result.discount_value, base);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, discount_amount: newDiscount }));[39m
[31m+       } catch (err) {[39m
[31m+         console.error("Error validating discount code:", err);[39m
[31m+         setDiscountCodeError("驗證折扣碼失敗");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }, 500);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     return () => clearTimeout(timer);[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, editForm.discount_code]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Auto-sync total_price when editOrderRooms change[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing || editOrderRooms.length === 0) return;[39m
[31m+     const roomsTotal = editOrderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+     if (roomsTotal !== editForm.total_price) {[39m
[31m+       setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, total_price: roomsTotal }));[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, editOrderRooms]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Re-initialize editOrderRooms when orderRooms load after editing already started[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing || orderRooms.length === 0 || editOrderRooms.length > 0) return;[39m
[31m+     const savedRoomsTotal = orderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+     // 以「純房費」為準（total_price 含加購，直接拿來當房費會重複計入加購而誤觸金額鎖）[39m
[31m+     const savedRoomFee = pureRoomFeeOf(order);[39m
[31m+     const shouldUseOrderTotalForSingleRoom = orderRooms.length === 1 && savedRoomFee > 0 && savedRoomsTotal !== savedRoomFee;[39m
[31m+     const normalizedRooms = orderRooms.map((or) => {[39m
[31m+       const correctedRoomPrice = shouldUseOrderTotalForSingleRoom ? savedRoomFee : Number(or.room_price || 0);[39m
[31m+       const unitPrice = getRoomUnitPrice(correctedRoomPrice, or.quantity);[39m
[31m+       return {[39m
[31m+         id: or.id,[39m
[31m+         room_type_id: or.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+         quantity: or.quantity,[39m
[31m+         room_price: correctedRoomPrice,[39m
[31m+         unit_price: unitPrice,[39m
[31m+         template_id: or.template_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         template_name: or.template_name,[39m
[31m+         room_unit_id: or.room_unit_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+       };[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     setEditOrderRooms(normalizedRooms);[39m
[31m+     const roomsTotal = normalizedRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+     setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, total_price: roomsTotal }));[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, orderRooms]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // Legacy fallback: if editing an order that has no order_rooms but has a chosen room_type_id,[39m
[31m+   // auto-populate a synthetic editOrderRoom so the new UI (with 棟號/房號 selector) is shown.[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (!isEditing || !order || !orderRoomsLoaded) return;[39m
[31m+     if (editOrderRooms.length > 0 || orderRooms.length > 0) return;[39m
[31m+     if (!editForm.room_type_id) return;[39m
[31m+     const syntheticPrice = Number(order.total_price || 0);[39m
[31m+     setEditOrderRooms([{[39m
[31m+       id: `synthetic-${order.id}`,[39m
[31m+       room_type_id: editForm.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+       quantity: 1,[39m
[31m+       room_price: syntheticPrice,[39m
[31m+       unit_price: syntheticPrice,[39m
[31m+       template_id: null,[39m
[31m+       template_name: null,[39m
[31m+       room_unit_id: null,[39m
[31m+     }]);[39m
[31m+   }, [isEditing, order, editForm.room_type_id, orderRooms.length, editOrderRooms.length, orderRoomsLoaded]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     if (embedded) return; // Ignore URL editing flag in embedded (tab) mode[39m
[31m+     if (searchParams.get("editing") === "true" && order && !isEditing && (orderRoomsLoaded || orderRoomsLoadError)) {[39m
[31m+       // 等 order_rooms 讀取完成（或確定失敗）才進入編輯，避免以 0 筆合成假房間[39m
[31m+       startEditing();[39m
[31m+       const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);[39m
[31m+       next.delete("editing");[39m
[31m+       setSearchParams(next, { replace: true });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   }, [searchParams, order, orderRooms, embedded, orderRoomsLoaded, orderRoomsLoadError]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const cancelEditing = () => {[39m
[31m+     setIsEditing(false);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSaveEdit = async () => {[39m
[31m+     // 合成列的選擇只用於這一次存檔：先取出再清空，任何結束路徑（成功、失敗、中途返回）都不會沿用[39m
[31m+     const syntheticDecision = syntheticDecisionRef.current;[39m
[31m+     syntheticDecisionRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     if (!order || isSavingEdit || savingEditRef.current) return;[39m
[31m+     // 硬守門：任何 orders / order_rooms 寫入前先擋掉無編輯權限者[39m
[31m+     if (!canManageOrders) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "權限不足",[39m
[31m+         description: "您目前對「訂單」模組僅有檢視權限，無法儲存變更。",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // 團購／信用卡訂單不得由內嵌編輯直接改成「已取消」，必須走專屬取消流程[39m
[31m+     if ([39m
[31m+       editForm.order_status === "cancelled" &&[39m
[31m+       order.order_status !== "cancelled" &&[39m
[31m+       isCancelBlockedOrder(order as any)[39m
[31m+     ) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "此訂單不能在這裡取消",[39m
[31m+         description: buildCancelBlockHint(order as any),[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     const roomsAction = decideOrderRoomsSave({[39m
[31m+       roomsLoaded: orderRoomsLoaded,[39m
[31m+       savedRoomCount: orderRooms.length,[39m
[31m+       editRoomIds: editOrderRooms.map(r => r.id),[39m
[31m+       syntheticDecision,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     if (roomsAction.kind === "block") {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "房間明細不可為 0 間",[39m
[31m+         description: "此訂單原本有房間明細，請至少保留一間房再儲存；若要取消訂單請改用取消流程。",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (roomsAction.kind === "confirm_synthetic") {[39m
[31m+       setSyntheticRoomPrompt(true);[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     // V5.5C.1：本次存檔正要把付款狀態改成「已付清」，但已付金額低於應收 → 先問清楚[39m
[31m+     if (!paidSyncOverrideRef.current) {[39m
[31m+       const FULLY_PAID = ["paid", "fully_paid"];[39m
[31m+       const savedTotalForCheck = editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total;[39m
[31m+       const calcFinalForCheck = savedTotalForCheck - editForm.discount_amount;[39m
[31m+       const pricingUnchangedForCheck =[39m
[31m+         Number(savedTotalForCheck) === Number(order.total_price ?? 0) &&[39m
[31m+         Number(editForm.discount_amount) === Number(order.discount_amount ?? 0) &&[39m
[31m+         Number(editForm.addon_total) === Number(savedAddonTotalOf(order));[39m
[31m+       const expectedFinal = pricingUnchangedForCheck ? Number(order.final_price ?? 0) : calcFinalForCheck;[39m
[31m+       const willBeFullyPaid = FULLY_PAID.includes(editForm.payment_status);[39m
[31m+       const wasFullyPaid = FULLY_PAID.includes(order.payment_status);[39m
[31m+       if (willBeFullyPaid && !wasFullyPaid && Number(editForm.paid_amount) < expectedFinal) {[39m
[31m+         setPaidSyncPrompt({ expected: expectedFinal, current: Number(editForm.paid_amount) });[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     savingEditRef.current = true;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // KOL 互惠額度檢查（編輯時：若來源改為/維持 'kol'，依入住日歸屬月份）[39m
[31m+     let kolOverrideStatus: KolQuotaStatus | null = null;[39m
[31m+     const kolPermitted = await ensureKolQuotaPermit({[39m
[31m+       accommodationId: order.accommodation_id,[39m
[31m+       orderSource: editForm.order_source,[39m
[31m+       checkInDate: order.check_in_date,[39m
[31m+       packageId: (editForm as any).package_id ?? (order as any).package_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+       packageIds: [[39m
[31m+         (editForm as any).package_id ?? (order as any).package_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         order?.room_type_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         ...editOrderRooms[39m
[31m+           .filter((r) => roomTypes.find((t) => t.id === r.room_type_id)?.is_exclusive_package)[39m
[31m+           .map((r) => r.room_type_id),[39m
[31m+       ],[39m
[31m+       excludeOrderId: order.id,[39m
[31m+       isAdmin: isAdminUser,[39m
[31m+       confirmOverride: (status) =>[39m
[31m+         new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => {[39m
[31m+           setKolQuotaPrompt({[39m
[31m+             yearMonth: status.yearMonth,[39m
[31m+             used: status.used,[39m
[31m+             quota: status.quota!,[39m
[31m+             packageName: status.packageName,[39m
[31m+             resolve,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }),[39m
[31m+       onOverride: (status) => { kolOverrideStatus = status; },[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     if (!kolPermitted) { savingEditRef.current = false; return; }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     setIsSavingEdit(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const savedTotalPrice = editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total;[39m
[31m+       const editFinalPriceCalc = savedTotalPrice - editForm.discount_amount;[39m
[31m+       // V5.5C.1：使用者在提示框做出的選擇（補足已付金額 / 改回已付訂金）優先於表單值[39m
[31m+       const effPaymentStatus = paidSyncOverrideRef.current?.payment_status ?? editForm.payment_status;[39m
[31m+       const effPaidAmount = paidSyncOverrideRef.current?.paid_amount ?? editForm.paid_amount;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // If any edited room is an exclusive package, regenerate one [包區/包棟房間配置] line per 包棟[39m
[31m+       const exclusiveRows = editOrderRooms.filter(r => roomTypes.find(t => t.id === r.room_type_id)?.is_exclusive_package);[39m
[31m+       let finalNotes = editForm.notes.trim();[39m
[31m+       // 先移除舊的加購備註區塊（多行格式）與舊的單行格式[39m
[31m+       finalNotes = finalNotes[39m
[31m+         .replace(/【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)[^】]*】\n?[\s\S]*?\n?【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)END】\n?/g, "")[39m
[31m+         .replace(/【額外現場加購金額[^】]*】\n?[\s\S]*?\n?【額外現場加購金額END】\n?/g, "")[39m
[31m+         .trim();[39m
[31m+       finalNotes = finalNotes[39m
[31m+         .split("\n")[39m
[31m+         .filter(line => !/^【\s*(?:包區|包棟)\s*房間配置\s*】/.test(line.trim()))[39m
[31m+         .filter(line => !/^【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)[^】]*】/.test(line.trim()))[39m
[31m+         .join("\n")[39m
[31m+         .trim();[39m
[31m+       // 以區塊格式寫入最新加購備註（支援多行）[39m
[31m+       const addonNoteTrim = editForm.addon_note.trim();[39m
[31m+       if (addonNoteTrim) {[39m
[31m+         const ts = new Date().toLocaleString("sv-SE", { timeZone: "Asia/Taipei" }).slice(0, 16);[39m
[31m+         const block = `【額外現場加購備註 ${ts}】\n${addonNoteTrim}\n【額外現場加購備註END】`;[39m
[31m+         finalNotes = finalNotes ? `${finalNotes}\n${block}` : block;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       // 寫入額外現場加購金額（純數字區塊）[39m
[31m+       if ((editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0) > 0) {[39m
[31m+         const ts2 = new Date().toLocaleString("sv-SE", { timeZone: "Asia/Taipei" }).slice(0, 16);[39m
[31m+         const amtBlock = `【額外現場加購金額 ${ts2}】\n${editForm.extra_addon_amount}\n【額外現場加購金額END】`;[39m
[31m+         finalNotes = finalNotes ? `${finalNotes}\n${amtBlock}` : amtBlock;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (exclusiveRows.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+         const pkgLines = exclusiveRows.map((_, i) => {[39m
[31m+           const cfg = editExclusiveConfigs[i] || {};[39m
[31m+           const parts = getBreakdownItemsFor(exclusiveRows[i]?.room_type_id)[39m
[31m+             .map(b => ({ name: b.name, n: Number(cfg[b.name]) || 0 }))[39m
[31m+             .filter(p => p.n > 0)[39m
[31m+             .map(p => `${p.name} ${p.n} 間`);[39m
[31m+           if (parts.length === 0) return "";[39m
[31m+           return `【${exclusiveLabel}房間配置】${parts.join("、")}`;[39m
[31m+         }).filter(line => line.length > 0);[39m
[31m+         if (pkgLines.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+           finalNotes = finalNotes ? `${pkgLines.join("\n")}\n${finalNotes}` : pkgLines.join("\n");[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const isPaidOrDeposit = ["paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status);[39m
[31m+       const originalAddonTotal = savedAddonTotalOf(order);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Block disallowed financial edits with friendly toast[39m
[31m+       // 注意：total_price 內含加購金額，因此只比對「純房費」部分，[39m
[31m+       // 讓已結清訂單仍可調整加購項目（DB trigger 亦採相同判斷）。[39m
[31m+       // 舊訂單的 total_price 可能未含加購，改用與編輯欄位相同的「純房費」口徑，避免誤擋。[39m
[31m+       const originalRoomFee = pureRoomFeeOf(order);[39m
[31m+       const blocked: string[] = [];[39m
[31m+       // super_admin 可修改已結清訂單的房費（DB trigger 亦放行）[39m
[31m+       if (isPaidOrDeposit && !isSuperAdmin && Number(editForm.total_price) !== Number(originalRoomFee)) blocked.push("房費金額");[39m
[31m+       if (isPaidOrDeposit && Number(editForm.discount_amount) !== Number(order.discount_amount ?? 0)) blocked.push("折扣金額");[39m
[31m+       if (isPaidOrDeposit && (editForm.discount_code.trim() || "") !== (order.discount_code || "")) blocked.push("折扣碼");[39m
[31m+       // v5.5：已結清訂單的加購一律走 update_paid_order_addons_v2，一般 Save 不得夾帶加購值[39m
[31m+       if (isPaidOrDeposit) {[39m
[31m+         const savedExtra = extractExtraAddonAmount(order.notes || "");[39m
[31m+         const savedNote = extractAddonNote(order.notes || "");[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editForm.addon_total) !== Number(savedAddonTotalOf(order))) blocked.push("加購金額");[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0) !== Number(savedExtra || 0)) blocked.push("額外現場加購金額");[39m
[31m+         if ((editForm.addon_note || "").trim() !== (savedNote || "").trim()) blocked.push("額外現場加購備註");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (blocked.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "已付款訂單無法修改金額",[39m
[31m+           description: `${blocked.join("、")} 無法在此處變更。折扣請改用「修改已結清折扣」，加購請改用「修改額外加購」，其餘請以退款或新增訂單方式處理。`,[39m
[31m+           variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         setIsSavingEdit(false);[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Auto-append locked audit line when a settled order has financial changes via inline edit[39m
[31m+       let auditNoteLine: string | null = null;[39m
[31m+       if (isPaidOrDeposit) {[39m
[31m+         const auditParts: string[] = [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // Addon diffs (寵物 / 餐食 / 自訂加購) — show item-level changes that explain 實收 變動[39m
[31m+         const oldPetTotal = (() => {[39m
[31m+           const all = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+           const legacyPet = all.find((a: any) => a?.addon_type === "寵物" || a?.addon_type === "pet");[39m
[31m+           return (order.pet_plan_price || 0) > 0 ? (order.pet_plan_price || 0) : (legacyPet?.total || 0);[39m
[31m+         })();[39m
[31m+         const oldPetCount = (() => {[39m
[31m+           const all = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+           const legacyPet = all.find((a: any) => a?.addon_type === "寵物" || a?.addon_type === "pet");[39m
[31m+           return (order.pet_count || 0) > 0 ? (order.pet_count || 0) : (legacyPet?.count || 0);[39m
[31m+         })();[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editForm.pet_count) !== Number(oldPetCount) || Number(editForm.pet_plan_price) !== Number(oldPetTotal)) {[39m
[31m+           auditParts.push(`寵物 ${oldPetCount}隻/${oldPetTotal}→${editForm.pet_count}隻/${editForm.pet_plan_price}`);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editForm.meal_plan_total) !== Number(order.meal_plan_total ?? 0)) {[39m
[31m+           auditParts.push(`餐食 ${order.meal_plan_total ?? 0}→${editForm.meal_plan_total}`);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         // Per-addon diff for custom_addon_details (excluding pet/meal which are tracked separately above)[39m
[31m+         const oldAddons: CustomAddonDetail[] = ((order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [])[39m
[31m+           .filter(a => a.addon_type !== "寵物" && a.addon_type !== "pet" && a.addon_type !== "餐食" && a.addon_type !== "meal");[39m
[31m+         const newAddons = editForm.custom_addon_details[39m
[31m+           .filter(a => a.addon_type !== "寵物" && a.addon_type !== "pet" && a.addon_type !== "餐食" && a.addon_type !== "meal");[39m
[31m+         const addonKeys = Array.from(new Set([...oldAddons.map(a => a.addon_type), ...newAddons.map(a => a.addon_type)]));[39m
[31m+         for (const k of addonKeys) {[39m
[31m+           const o = oldAddons.find(a => a.addon_type === k);[39m
[31m+           const n = newAddons.find(a => a.addon_type === k);[39m
[31m+           const oc = o?.count || 0, ot = o?.total || 0;[39m
[31m+           const nc = n?.count || 0, nt = n?.total || 0;[39m
[31m+           if (oc !== nc || ot !== nt) {[39m
[31m+             auditParts.push(`${k} ${oc}/${ot}→${nc}/${nt}`);[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editFinalPriceCalc) !== Number(order.final_price ?? 0)) {[39m
[31m+           auditParts.push(`實收 ${order.final_price}→${editFinalPriceCalc}`);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editForm.deposit_amount) !== Number(order.deposit_amount ?? 0)) {[39m
[31m+           auditParts.push(`訂金 ${order.deposit_amount ?? 0}→${editForm.deposit_amount}`);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (Number(effPaidAmount) !== Number(order.paid_amount ?? 0)) {[39m
[31m+           auditParts.push(`已付 ${order.paid_amount ?? 0}→${effPaidAmount}`);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (auditParts.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+           const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();[39m
[31m+           let who = "unknown";[39m
[31m+           if (user) {[39m
[31m+             const { data: prof } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("name").eq("id", user.id).maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+             who = (prof?.name && prof.name.trim()) || user.email || user.id;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           const ts = new Date().toLocaleString("sv-SE", { timeZone: "Asia/Taipei" }).slice(0, 16);[39m
[31m+           auditNoteLine = `🔒[金額異動 ${ts} by ${who}] ${auditParts.join("、")}`;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const updateData: Record<string, any> = buildInlineEditPatch({[39m
[31m+         order: {[39m
[31m+           payment_status: order.payment_status,[39m
[31m+           total_price: order.total_price,[39m
[31m+           discount_amount: order.discount_amount,[39m
[31m+           discount_code: order.discount_code,[39m
[31m+           final_price: order.final_price,[39m
[31m+         },[39m
[31m+         form: editForm as any,[39m
[31m+         isOlivia,[39m
[31m+         isHoldOrder: isHoldOrder(order),[39m
[31m+         effPaymentStatus,[39m
[31m+         effPaidAmount,[39m
[31m+         savedTotalPrice,[39m
[31m+         editFinalPriceCalc,[39m
[31m+         originalAddonTotal,[39m
[31m+         finalNotes,[39m
[31m+         auditNoteLine,[39m
[31m+         nowIso: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+         originalOrderStatus: order.order_status,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 進入編輯後若訂單已被其他人或系統更新，停止存檔（此時尚未寫入任何資料）[39m
[31m+       const { data: freshOrder, error: freshError } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("orders")[39m
[31m+         .select("updated_at")[39m
[31m+         .eq("id", order.id)[39m
[31m+         .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+       if (freshError) throw freshError;[39m
[31m+       if (!freshOrder) throw new Error("找不到訂單，請重新整理");[39m
[31m+       if (openedUpdatedAtRef.current && (freshOrder as any).updated_at !== openedUpdatedAtRef.current) {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "訂單已被更新，未儲存",[39m
[31m+           description: "您進入編輯後，這張訂單已被其他人或系統更新。請重新整理頁面後再編輯。",[39m
[31m+           variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase.rpc("update_order_fields_v1", {[39m
[31m+         p_order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         p_patch: updateData as any,[39m
[31m+         p_source: "admin_order_inline_edit",[39m
[31m+         p_reason: null,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 第二步失敗時，第一步的欄位已存檔，不可再顯示「儲存失敗」[39m
[31m+       let roomsSynced = false;[39m
[31m+       let roomsSyncFailure: string | null = null;[39m
[31m+       // V5.5A.9: order_rooms 改由 sync_order_rooms_v1 以 diff 方式在單一交易內同步[39m
[31m+       if (roomsAction.kind === "sync") try {[39m
[31m+         // 自動安排：對於 quantity===1 且未手動指派的列，依 display_order 找出可用 unit[39m
[31m+         const needsAuto = editOrderRooms.filter(r => r.quantity === 1 && !r.room_unit_id);[39m
[31m+         const autoAssignMap = new Map<string, string>();[39m
[31m+         if (needsAuto.length > 0 && order.accommodation_id) {[39m
[31m+           const typeIds = Array.from(new Set(needsAuto.map(r => r.room_type_id)));[39m
[31m+           const [unitsRes, occRes] = await Promise.all([[39m
[31m+             supabase[39m
[31m+               .from("room_units" as any)[39m
[31m+               .select("id, room_type_id, display_order, is_active")[39m
[31m+               .eq("accommodation_id", order.accommodation_id)[39m
[31m+               .eq("is_active", true)[39m
[31m+               .in("room_type_id", typeIds)[39m
[31m+               .order("display_order", { ascending: true }),[39m
[31m+             supabase[39m
[31m+               .from("order_rooms")[39m
[31m+               .select("room_unit_id, orders!inner(id, accommodation_id, order_status, check_in_date, check_out_date)")[39m
[31m+               .not("room_unit_id", "is", null)[39m
[31m+               .eq("orders.accommodation_id", order.accommodation_id)[39m
[31m+               .neq("orders.order_status", "cancelled")[39m
[31m+               .lt("orders.check_in_date", order.check_out_date)[39m
[31m+               .gt("orders.check_out_date", order.check_in_date),[39m
[31m+           ]);[39m
[31m+           const reserved = new Set<string>();[39m
[31m+           ((occRes.data as any[]) || []).forEach((row: any) => {[39m
[31m+             if (row.room_unit_id) reserved.add(row.room_unit_id);[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           // 排除本次手動指派[39m
[31m+           editOrderRooms.forEach(r => { if (r.room_unit_id) reserved.add(r.room_unit_id); });[39m
[31m+           const units = (((unitsRes.data as any[]) || [])).sort((a: any, b: any) => a.display_order - b.display_order);[39m
[31m+           needsAuto.forEach(r => {[39m
[31m+             const u = units.find((x: any) => x.room_type_id === r.room_type_id && !reserved.has(x.id));[39m
[31m+             if (u) {[39m
[31m+               autoAssignMap.set(r.id, u.id);[39m
[31m+               reserved.add(u.id);[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const existingIds = new Set((orderRooms || []).map((o: any) => o.id));[39m
[31m+         const payloadRooms = editOrderRooms.map(r => ({[39m
[31m+           // synthetic / 新增列不帶 id，讓 RPC 走 INSERT[39m
[31m+           id: existingIds.has(r.id) ? r.id : null,[39m
[31m+           room_type_id: r.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+           quantity: r.quantity,[39m
[31m+           room_price: r.room_price,[39m
[31m+           // template_id 是折扣碼適用範圍的唯一依據；沒帶就會被 RPC 寫成 NULL[39m
[31m+           template_id: resolveTemplateId(allTemplates as any, r.room_type_id, r.template_name, r.template_id),[39m
[31m+           template_name: r.template_name,[39m
[31m+           room_unit_id: r.quantity !== 1 ? null : (r.room_unit_id || autoAssignMap.get(r.id) || null),[39m
[31m+         }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 以第一步存檔後的 updated_at 作為樂觀鎖；中間若被更新，RPC 會回 STALE_ORDER[39m
[31m+         const { data: afterFields, error: afterFieldsError } = await supabase[39m
[31m+           .from("orders")[39m
[31m+           .select("updated_at")[39m
[31m+           .eq("id", order.id)[39m
[31m+           .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+         if (afterFieldsError) throw afterFieldsError;[39m
[31m+         if (!(afterFields as any)?.updated_at) throw new Error("讀不到訂單更新時間");[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const { data: syncResult, error: syncError } = await supabase.rpc("sync_order_rooms_v1" as any, {[39m
[31m+           p_order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+           p_expected_updated_at: (afterFields as any).updated_at,[39m
[31m+           p_rooms: payloadRooms as any,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         if (syncError) throw syncError;[39m
[31m+         roomsSynced = true;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         const conflicts = ((syncResult as any)?.conflicts ?? []) as any[];[39m
[31m+         if (conflicts.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+           toast({[39m
[31m+             title: "部分房號未指派",[39m
[31m+             description: `有 ${conflicts.length} 間房的房號已被其他訂單佔用（${conflicts[39m
[31m+               .map((c: any) => c.conflict_order_number)[39m
[31m+               .filter(Boolean)[39m
[31m+               .join("、")}），已改為未指派，其餘資料已儲存。`,[39m
[31m+             variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch (syncErr: any) {[39m
[31m+         console.error("房間明細儲存失敗:", syncErr);[39m
[31m+         const raw = String(syncErr?.message || syncErr?.error_description || "");[39m
[31m+         roomsSyncFailure = raw.includes("STALE_ORDER")[39m
[31m+           ? "儲存過程中，這張訂單被其他人或系統更新。"[39m
[31m+           : (raw || "請稍後再試。");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Audit log[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();[39m
[31m+         if (user) {[39m
[31m+           const changes = computeOrderDiff(order as any, updateData);[39m
[31m+           // 房間配置差異[39m
[31m+           const roomTypeNameMap: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries([39m
[31m+             (roomTypes || []).map((t: any) => [t.id, t.name])[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           const unitNameMap: Record<string, string> = Object.fromEntries([39m
[31m+             (roomUnits || []).map((u: any) => [u.id, u.nickname || u.unit_number])[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           const roomsAfter = editOrderRooms.map(r => ({[39m
[31m+             room_type_id: r.room_type_id,[39m
[31m+             quantity: r.quantity,[39m
[31m+             room_price: r.room_price,[39m
[31m+             room_unit_id: r.quantity !== 1 ? null : (r.room_unit_id || null),[39m
[31m+           }));[39m
[31m+           const roomsDiff = computeRoomsDiff([39m
[31m+             initialEditSnapshot.current.rooms,[39m
[31m+             roomsAfter,[39m
[31m+             roomTypeNameMap,[39m
[31m+             unitNameMap,[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           if (roomsDiff && roomsSynced) changes.push(roomsDiff);[39m
[31m+           const exclusiveDiff = computeExclusiveConfigDiff([39m
[31m+             initialEditSnapshot.current.exclusiveConfigs,[39m
[31m+             editExclusiveConfigs,[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           if (exclusiveDiff) changes.push(exclusiveDiff);[39m
[31m+           const notesDiff = computeVisibleNotesDiff(order.notes, updateData.notes);[39m
[31m+           if (notesDiff) changes.push(notesDiff);[39m
[31m+           const addonNoteBefore = extractAddonNote(order.notes);[39m
[31m+           const addonNoteAfter = extractAddonNote(updateData.notes);[39m
[31m+           if (addonNoteBefore !== addonNoteAfter) {[39m
[31m+             changes.push({ field: "addon_note", label: "額外現場加購備註", before: addonNoteBefore || "(空)", after: addonNoteAfter || "(空)" });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           const extraAddonBefore = extractExtraAddonAmount(order.notes);[39m
[31m+           if (Number(extraAddonBefore) !== Number(editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0)) {[39m
[31m+             changes.push({ field: "extra_addon_amount", label: "額外現場加購金額", before: extraAddonBefore, after: editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0 });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           // trigger 已涵蓋 orders 欄位；此處只補記 order_rooms／包區設定／[39m
[31m+           // 額外加購等不在 orders 資料表的內容（V5.5C.2）[39m
[31m+           const NON_ORDER_FIELDS = ["rooms", "exclusive_configs", "addon_note", "extra_addon_amount"];[39m
[31m+           const residual = changes.filter((c: any) => NON_ORDER_FIELDS.includes(c.field));[39m
[31m+           if (residual.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+             const { error: auditError } = await supabase.from("audit_logs").insert([{[39m
[31m+               user_id: user.id,[39m
[31m+               user_email: user.email,[39m
[31m+               action_type: "update",[39m
[31m+               action_category: "order",[39m
[31m+               entity_type: "order",[39m
[31m+               entity_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+               entity_name: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+               accommodation_id: order.accommodation_id,[39m
[31m+               details: { type: "inline_edit", changes: residual } as any,[39m
[31m+             }]);[39m
[31m+             if (auditError) throw auditError;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+         console.error("Audit log error:", e);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (kolOverrideStatus && order.accommodation_id) {[39m
[31m+         await logKolQuotaOverride({[39m
[31m+           orderId: order.id,[39m
[31m+           orderNumber: order.order_number,[39m
[31m+           accommodationId: order.accommodation_id,[39m
[31m+           status: kolOverrideStatus,[39m
[31m+           context: "edit",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (roomsSyncFailure) {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "訂單欄位已儲存，房間明細未儲存",[39m
[31m+           description: `${roomsSyncFailure}請重新整理頁面確認後，再修改房間明細。`,[39m
[31m+           variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       } else if (roomsAction.kind === "skip" && roomsAction.reason === "synthetic_skipped") {[39m
[31m+         toast({ title: "儲存成功", description: "訂單已更新（未寫入房間明細）" });[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         toast({ title: "儲存成功", description: "訂單已更新" });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       setIsEditing(false);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Notify kol-webhook of potential discount-code change[39m
[31m+       try {[39m
[31m+         await supabase.functions.invoke('kol-webhook', {[39m
[31m+           body: {[39m
[31m+             order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+             trigger_type: "update",[39m
[31m+             previous_discount_code: originalDiscountCode.current ?? null,[39m
[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       } catch (e) {[39m
[31m+         console.error("kol-webhook invoke (update) error:", e);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       fetchOrder();[39m
[31m+     } catch (error: any) {[39m
[31m+       console.error("儲存失敗:", error);[39m
[31m+       if (isCancelBlockedError(error)) {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "此訂單不能在這裡取消",[39m
[31m+           description: buildCancelBlockHint(order as any),[39m
[31m+           variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         const msg = error?.message || error?.error_description || "請稍後再試";[39m
[31m+         toast({ title: "儲存失敗", description: msg, variant: "destructive" });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setIsSavingEdit(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       savingEditRef.current = false;[39m
[31m+       paidSyncOverrideRef.current = null;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleCheckinAction = async (action: "checkin" | "undo_checkin" | "checkout" | "undo_checkout") => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const patch = buildCheckinActionPatch({[39m
[31m+         action,[39m
[31m+         nowIso: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+         hasCheckedInAt: !!order.checked_in_at,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase.rpc("update_order_fields_v1", {[39m
[31m+         p_order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         p_patch: patch as any,[39m
[31m+         p_source: `admin_${action}`,[39m
[31m+         p_reason: null,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       // 變更紀錄由 zzz_order_change_log trigger 統一寫入（V5.5C.2）[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title:[39m
[31m+           action === "checkin" ? "已辦理入住"[39m
[31m+             : action === "checkout" ? "已辦理退房"[39m
[31m+               : action === "undo_checkin" ? "已取消入住記錄"[39m
[31m+                 : "已取消退房記錄",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       fetchOrder();[39m
[31m+     } catch (err: any) {[39m
[31m+       toast({ title: "操作失敗", description: err.message, variant: "destructive" });[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const openPriceEditDialog = () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     setEditTotalPrice(order.total_price);[39m
[31m+     setEditDiscountAmount(order.discount_amount);[39m
[31m+     setEditDepositAmount(order.deposit_amount);[39m
[31m+     setEditPaidAmount(order.paid_amount);[39m
[31m+     setEditReason("");[39m
[31m+     setShowPriceEditDialog(true);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const editFinalPrice = editTotalPrice - editDiscountAmount;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSavePriceEdit = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     if (editFinalPrice < 0) {[39m
[31m+       toast({ title: "實收金額不可為負數", variant: "destructive" });[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     setIsSavingPrice(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       let newPaymentStatus = order.payment_status;[39m
[31m+       if (editPaidAmount <= 0) {[39m
[31m+         newPaymentStatus = "unpaid";[39m
[31m+       } else if (editPaidAmount >= editFinalPrice) {[39m
[31m+         newPaymentStatus = "paid";[39m
[31m+       } else if (editPaidAmount > 0 && editPaidAmount < editFinalPrice) {[39m
[31m+         newPaymentStatus = "deposit_paid";[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // Build locked audit-line when this is a paid/deposit-paid order with amount changes[39m
[31m+       const wasPaidOrDeposit = ["paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status);[39m
[31m+       const amountChanged =[39m
[31m+         Number(editTotalPrice) !== Number(order.total_price ?? 0) ||[39m
[31m+         Number(editDiscountAmount) !== Number(order.discount_amount ?? 0) ||[39m
[31m+         Number(editDepositAmount) !== Number(order.deposit_amount ?? 0) ||[39m
[31m+         Number(editPaidAmount) !== Number(order.paid_amount ?? 0) ||[39m
[31m+         Number(editFinalPrice) !== Number(order.final_price ?? 0);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       let nextNotes: string | null = order.notes ?? null;[39m
[31m+       if (wasPaidOrDeposit && amountChanged) {[39m
[31m+         const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();[39m
[31m+         let who = "unknown";[39m
[31m+         if (user) {[39m
[31m+           const { data: prof } = await supabase.from("profiles").select("name").eq("id", user.id).maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+           who = (prof?.name && prof.name.trim()) || user.email || user.id;[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         const ts = new Date().toLocaleString("sv-SE", { timeZone: "Asia/Taipei" }).slice(0, 16);[39m
[31m+         const parts: string[] = [];[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editTotalPrice) !== Number(order.total_price ?? 0)) parts.push(`房費 ${order.total_price}→${editTotalPrice}`);[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editDiscountAmount) !== Number(order.discount_amount ?? 0)) parts.push(`折扣 ${order.discount_amount}→${editDiscountAmount}`);[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editDepositAmount) !== Number(order.deposit_amount ?? 0)) parts.push(`訂金 ${order.deposit_amount ?? 0}→${editDepositAmount}`);[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editPaidAmount) !== Number(order.paid_amount ?? 0)) parts.push(`已付 ${order.paid_amount ?? 0}→${editPaidAmount}`);[39m
[31m+         if (Number(editFinalPrice) !== Number(order.final_price ?? 0)) parts.push(`實收 ${order.final_price}→${editFinalPrice}`);[39m
[31m+         const reasonStr = editReason.trim() ? `｜原因：${editReason.trim()}` : "";[39m
[31m+         const auditLine = `🔒[金額異動 ${ts} by ${who}] ${parts.join("、")}${reasonStr}`;[39m
[31m+         nextNotes = nextNotes ? `${nextNotes}\n${auditLine}` : auditLine;[39m
[31m+       } else if (editReason.trim()) {[39m
[31m+         nextNotes = order.notes[39m
[31m+           ? `${order.notes}\n\n【金額修改】${editReason.trim()}`[39m
[31m+           : `【金額修改】${editReason.trim()}`;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase.rpc("update_order_fields_v1", {[39m
[31m+         p_order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         p_patch: buildAdminPriceEditPatch({[39m
[31m+           totalPrice: editTotalPrice,[39m
[31m+           discountAmount: editDiscountAmount,[39m
[31m+           finalPrice: editFinalPrice,[39m
[31m+           depositAmount: editDepositAmount,[39m
[31m+           paidAmount: editPaidAmount,[39m
[31m+           paymentStatus: newPaymentStatus,[39m
[31m+           notes: nextNotes,[39m
[31m+         }) as any,[39m
[31m+         p_source: "admin_price_edit",[39m
[31m+         p_reason: editReason.trim() || null,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       // 變更紀錄由 trigger 統一寫入；修改原因已寫入 notes 的稽核行（V5.5C.2）[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       toast({ title: "金額修改成功", description: "訂單金額已更新" });[39m
[31m+       setShowPriceEditDialog(false);[39m
[31m+       fetchOrder();[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       console.error("修改金額失敗:", error);[39m
[31m+       toast({ title: "修改失敗", description: "無法修改訂單金額，請稍後再試", variant: "destructive" });[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setIsSavingPrice(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const fetchOrder = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!orderId) return;[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       setLoading(true);[39m
[31m+       const { data, error } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("orders")[39m
[31m+         .select(`[39m
[31m+           *,[39m
[31m+           room_types ([39m
[31m+             name,[39m
[31m+             name_en[39m
[31m+           )[39m
[31m+         `)[39m
[31m+         .eq("id", orderId)[39m
[31m+         .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       if (data) {[39m
[31m+         const orderData = data as any;[39m
[31m+         const orderObj = {[39m
[31m+           ...orderData,[39m
[31m+           pet_count: orderData.pet_count || 0,[39m
[31m+           pet_plan_price: orderData.pet_plan_price || 0,[39m
[31m+           meal_plan_details: (orderData.meal_plan_details || []).filter((d: any) => d && d.date),[39m
[31m+           meal_plan_total: orderData.meal_plan_total || 0,[39m
[31m+         } as OrderDetail;[39m
[31m+         setOrder(orderObj);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // Fire all dependent lookups in parallel — they only need orderData.[39m
[31m+         const accId = orderData.accommodation_id;[39m
[31m+         const [[39m
[31m+           orderRoomsRes,[39m
[31m+           rtRes,[39m
[31m+           unitsRes,[39m
[31m+           occupiedRes,[39m
[31m+           templatesRes,[39m
[31m+           holidayRes,[39m
[31m+           addonRes,[39m
[31m+         ] = await Promise.all([[39m
[31m+           supabase[39m
[31m+             .from("order_rooms")[39m
[31m+             .select("id, room_type_id, quantity, room_price, template_id, template_name, room_unit_id, room_types(name), room_units(unit_number, nickname)")[39m
[31m+             .eq("order_id", orderId),[39m
[31m+           accId[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("room_types")[39m
[31m+                 .select("id, name, base_price, is_exclusive_package")[39m
[31m+                 .eq("accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .order("display_order", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+           accId[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("room_units" as any)[39m
[31m+                 .select("id, room_type_id, unit_number, nickname, is_active")[39m
[31m+                 .eq("accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .eq("is_active", true)[39m
[31m+                 .order("display_order", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+           accId && orderData.check_in_date && orderData.check_out_date[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("order_rooms")[39m
[31m+                 .select("room_unit_id, orders!inner(id, order_number, order_status, check_in_date, check_out_date)")[39m
[31m+                 .not("room_unit_id", "is", null)[39m
[31m+                 .neq("order_id", orderId)[39m
[31m+                 .eq("orders.accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .neq("orders.order_status", "cancelled")[39m
[31m+                 .lt("orders.check_in_date", orderData.check_out_date)[39m
[31m+                 .gt("orders.check_out_date", orderData.check_in_date)[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+           accId[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("pricing_templates")[39m
[31m+                 .select("id, template_name, meal_plans, room_type_id, room_type_ids, price, weekday_price, peak_price, weekend_price, display_order")[39m
[31m+                 .eq("accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .order("display_order", { ascending: true, nullsFirst: false })[39m
[31m+                 .order("template_name", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+           accId && orderData.check_in_date && orderData.check_out_date[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("public_holidays")[39m
[31m+                 .select("holiday_date, holiday_type")[39m
[31m+                 .eq("accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .gte("holiday_date", orderData.check_in_date)[39m
[31m+                 .lte("holiday_date", orderData.check_out_date)[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+           accId[39m
[31m+             ? supabase[39m
[31m+                 .from("addon_settings")[39m
[31m+                 .select("id, addon_type, description, unit_label, price_per_unit, pricing_mode, min_quantity, max_quantity, enabled")[39m
[31m+                 .eq("accommodation_id", accId)[39m
[31m+                 .eq("enabled", true)[39m
[31m+                 .order("sort_order", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+                 .order("created_at", { ascending: true })[39m
[31m+             : Promise.resolve({ data: null } as any),[39m
[31m+         ]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 讀取失敗不可當成 0 筆：鎖住房間明細的編輯與儲存[39m
[31m+         if (orderRoomsRes.error) {[39m
[31m+           console.error("order_rooms 讀取失敗:", orderRoomsRes.error);[39m
[31m+           setOrderRooms([]);[39m
[31m+           setOrderRoomsLoaded(false);[39m
[31m+           setOrderRoomsLoadError(orderRoomsRes.error.message || "未知錯誤");[39m
[31m+         } else {[39m
[31m+           const orderRoomsData = orderRoomsRes.data;[39m
[31m+           setOrderRooms(orderRoomsData && orderRoomsData.length > 0 ? (orderRoomsData as unknown as OrderRoom[]) : []);[39m
[31m+           setOrderRoomsLoadError(null);[39m
[31m+           setOrderRoomsLoaded(true);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (accId) {[39m
[31m+           const rtData = rtRes.data as any[] | null;[39m
[31m+           if (rtData) setRoomTypes(rtData as any);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const unitsData = unitsRes.data as any[] | null;[39m
[31m+           if (unitsData) {[39m
[31m+             const occupiedMap = new Map<string, { id: string; order_number: string }>();[39m
[31m+             (((occupiedRes as any).data as any[]) || []).forEach((row: any) => {[39m
[31m+               const o = row.orders;[39m
[31m+               if (!o) return;[39m
[31m+               occupiedMap.set(row.room_unit_id, { id: o.id, order_number: o.order_number });[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+             setRoomUnits([39m
[31m+               (unitsData as any[]).map((u) => ({[39m
[31m+                 ...u,[39m
[31m+                 occupied_by_order_id: occupiedMap.get(u.id)?.id || null,[39m
[31m+                 occupied_by_order_number: occupiedMap.get(u.id)?.order_number || null,[39m
[31m+               })),[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const templatesData = templatesRes.data as any[] | null;[39m
[31m+           if (templatesData && templatesData.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+             const roomTypeId = orderData.room_type_id;[39m
[31m+             const roomTypeTemplates = roomTypeId[39m
[31m+               ? templatesData.filter((t: any) => templateMatchesRoomType(t, roomTypeId))[39m
[31m+               : templatesData;[39m
[31m+             setRoomTemplates(roomTypeTemplates as any);[39m
[31m+             setAllRoomTemplates(templatesData as any);[39m
[31m+             const derivedPlans: CustomMealPlan[] = [];[39m
[31m+             const defaultTemplate = roomTypeTemplates.find((t: any) => (t as any).meal_plans && Array.isArray((t as any).meal_plans) && ((t as any).meal_plans as any[]).length > 0);[39m
[31m+             if (defaultTemplate) {[39m
[31m+               const plans = (defaultTemplate as any).meal_plans as CustomMealPlan[];[39m
[31m+               derivedPlans.push(...plans);[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+             for (const t of roomTypeTemplates) {[39m
[31m+               const name = (t as any).template_name || "";[39m
[31m+               if (name.includes("二食") || name.includes("2食")) {[39m
[31m+                 if (!derivedPlans.some(p => p.id === "2_meals")) {[39m
[31m+                   derivedPlans.push({ id: "2_meals", name: "一泊二食", description: "" });[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+               if (name.includes("三食") || name.includes("3食")) {[39m
[31m+                 if (!derivedPlans.some(p => p.id === "3_meals")) {[39m
[31m+                   derivedPlans.push({ id: "3_meals", name: "一泊三食", description: "" });[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+             setCustomMealPlans(derivedPlans);[39m
[31m+             setAllTemplates(templatesData as any);[39m
[31m+           } else {[39m
[31m+             setRoomTemplates([]);[39m
[31m+             setAllRoomTemplates([]);[39m
[31m+             setCustomMealPlans([]);[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const holidayData = holidayRes.data as any[] | null;[39m
[31m+           if (holidayData) {[39m
[31m+             setPublicHolidays([39m
[31m+               holidayData[39m
[31m+                 .filter((h: any) => h.holiday_type !== "work_on_weekend")[39m
[31m+                 .map((h: any) => h.holiday_date),[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           const addonData = addonRes.data as any[] | null;[39m
[31m+           if (addonData) setAddonSettings(addonData as AddonSetting[]);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+       console.error("Error fetching order:", error);[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "載入失敗",[39m
[31m+         description: "無法載入訂單資料",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setLoading(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     fetchOrder();[39m
[31m+   }, [orderId]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getPaymentStatusBadge = (status: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const statusMap: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {[39m
[31m+       unpaid: { label: "未付款", className: "bg-error-light text-error border-error/20" },[39m
[31m+       deposit_paid: { label: "已付訂金", className: "bg-warning-light text-warning border-warning/20" },[39m
[31m+       paid: { label: "已付清", className: "bg-success-light text-success border-success/20" },[39m
[31m+       overdue: { label: "已逾期", className: "bg-error-light text-error border-error/20" },[39m
[31m+       refunded: { label: "已退款", className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border" },[39m
[31m+       partial_refunded: { label: "部分退款", className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border-amber-200" },[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const config = statusMap[status] || { label: status, className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border" };[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border ${config.className}`}>[39m
[31m+         {config.label}[39m
[31m+       </span>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getOrderStatusBadge = (status: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const statusMap: Record<string, { label: string; className: string }> = {[39m
[31m+       pending: { label: "待確認", className: "bg-warning-light text-warning border-warning/20" },[39m
[31m+       confirmed: { label: "已確認", className: "bg-primary/10 text-primary border-primary/20" },[39m
[31m+       checked_in: { label: "已入住", className: "bg-info-light text-info border-info/20" },[39m
[31m+       checked_out: { label: "已退房", className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border" },[39m
[31m+       cancelled: { label: "已取消", className: "bg-error-light text-error border-error/20" },[39m
[31m+       hold: { label: "保留中", className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border" },[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const config = statusMap[status] || { label: status, className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border" };[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border ${config.className}`}>[39m
[31m+         {config.label}[39m
[31m+       </span>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getOrderSourceBadge = (source: string) => {[39m
[31m+     const sourceMap: Record<string, { label: string; className: string; icon: typeof Globe }> = {[39m
[31m+       website: { label: "官網訂房", className: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       manual: { label: "現場訂房", className: "bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 border-purple-200", icon: UserPlus },[39m

[31m+       agoda: { label: "Agoda", className: "bg-green-100 text-green-700 border-green-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       booking: { label: "Booking", className: "bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       trip: { label: "Trip", className: "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 border-orange-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       travel_agency: { label: "旅行社", className: "bg-teal-100 text-teal-700 border-teal-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       peer_trade: { label: "同業", className: "bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border-teal-300", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       kol: { label: "KOL互惠", className: "bg-pink-100 text-pink-700 border-pink-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       points_starchain: { label: "星鏈會員", className: "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border-amber-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       official_use: { label: "公務使用", className: "bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border-slate-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       franchise_diff: { label: "加盟價差", className: "bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border-rose-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       owner_provided: { label: "業主自帶", className: "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 border-yellow-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+       hotelnabe: { label: "hotelnabe", className: "bg-sky-100 text-sky-700 border-sky-200", icon: Globe },[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     const config = sourceMap[source] || { label: source || "官網訂房", className: "bg-muted text-muted-foreground border-border", icon: Globe };[39m
[31m+     const Icon = config.icon;[39m
[31m+     // 旭海（hotelnabe）附加 OTA 通路來源，例如 hotelnabe - booking / hotelnabe - agoda[39m
[31m+     const otaRaw = (order as any)?.hotelnabe_sync_state?.ota_code;[39m
[31m+     const otaCode = typeof otaRaw === "string" ? otaRaw.trim().toLowerCase() : "";[39m
[31m+     const label = source === "hotelnabe" && otaCode ? `${config.label} - ${otaCode}` : config.label;[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       <TooltipProvider delayDuration={200}>[39m
[31m+         <Tooltip>[39m
[31m+           <TooltipTrigger asChild>[39m
[31m+             <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-xs font-medium border cursor-help ${config.className}`}>[39m
[31m+               <Icon className="w-3 h-3" />[39m
[31m+               {label}[39m
[31m+             </span>[39m
[31m+           </TooltipTrigger>[39m
[31m+           <TooltipContent side="top" className="max-w-xs">[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1 text-xs">[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex gap-2"><span className="text-muted-foreground">來源 code：</span><span className="font-mono">{source || "—"}</span></div>[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex gap-2"><span className="text-muted-foreground">顯示名稱：</span><span>{getOrderSourceDisplayName(source)}</span></div>[39m
[31m+               {otaCode && ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="border-t border-border/50 my-1" />[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex gap-2"><span className="text-muted-foreground">OTA code：</span><span className="font-mono">{otaCode}</span></div>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex gap-2"><span className="text-muted-foreground">OTA 通路：</span><span>{getHotelnabeOtaDisplayName(otaCode)}</span></div>[39m

[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </TooltipContent>[39m
[31m+         </Tooltip>[39m
[31m+       </TooltipProvider>[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getPaymentMethodDisplayName = (method: string | null) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!method) return "-";[39m
[31m+     const methodMap: Record<string, string> = {[39m
[31m+       credit_card: "信用卡",[39m
[31m+       credit_card_3ds: "信用卡",[39m
[31m+       credit_card_payuni: "信用卡(PAYUNi)",[39m
[31m+       atm: "ATM 虛擬帳號",[39m
[31m+       atm_payuni: "ATM 虛擬帳號(PAYUNi)",[39m
[31m+       line_pay: "LINE Pay",[39m

[31m+       cash: "現金",[39m
[31m+       monthly: "月結",[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+     return methodMap[method] || method;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getMealPlanDisplayName = (planId: string | null) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!planId) return "-";[39m
[31m+     const customPlan = customMealPlans.find(p => p.id === planId);[39m
[31m+     if (customPlan) {[39m
[31m+       return customPlan.name;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     return planId === "2_meals" ? "一泊二食" : planId === "3_meals" ? "一泊三食" : planId;[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const getTemplateDisplayName = (planId: string | null) => {[39m
[31m+     if (!planId) return "-";[39m
[31m+     // Prefer match by template_name (CreateOrderDialog / hold-edit store the name)[39m
[31m+     const byName = roomTemplates.find(t => t.template_name === planId) || allRoomTemplates.find(t => t.template_name === planId);[39m
[31m+     if (byName) return byName.template_name;[39m
[31m+     // Legacy: meal_plan was stored as template id (UUID)[39m
[31m+     const byId = roomTemplates.find(t => t.id === planId) || allRoomTemplates.find(t => t.id === planId);[39m
[31m+     if (byId) return byId.template_name;[39m
[31m+     return getMealPlanDisplayName(planId);[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   /**[39m
[31m+    * D2「專案」欄位顯示順序（只改顯示，不動 orders.meal_plan 寫入）：[39m
[31m+    *   orders.pricing_template_id 的專案名稱 → orders.package_id 的方案名稱 → 現行 meal_plan 顯示[39m
[31m+    */[39m
[31m+   const getOrderProjectLabel = () => {[39m
[31m+     const pkgId = (order as any)?.package_id ?? null;[39m
[31m+     return ([39m
[31m+       resolveOrderProjectDisplayName({[39m
[31m+         pricingTemplateId: (order as any)?.pricing_template_id ?? null,[39m
[31m+         templates: allRoomTemplates.length > 0 ? allRoomTemplates : roomTemplates,[39m
[31m+         packageId: pkgId,[39m
[31m+         packages: pkgId && orderPackageName ? [{ id: pkgId, name: orderPackageName }] : [],[39m
[31m+       }) ?? getTemplateDisplayName(order?.meal_plan ?? null)[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // D2 顯示用：載入 orders.package_id 的方案名稱（唯讀，不影響任何寫入）[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     const pkgId = (order as any)?.package_id ?? null;[39m
[31m+     if (!pkgId) {[39m
[31m+       setOrderPackageName(null);[39m
[31m+       return;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       const { data } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("accommodation_packages")[39m
[31m+         .select("id, name")[39m
[31m+         .eq("id", pkgId)[39m
[31m+         .maybeSingle();[39m
[31m+       if (!cancelled) setOrderPackageName(((data as any)?.name as string) ?? null);[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+     return () => {[39m
[31m+       cancelled = true;[39m
[31m+     };[39m
[31m+   }, [(order as any)?.package_id]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // 確認目前使用者是否為該訂單民宿之管理員（用於顯示「同步 TapPay 退款狀態」按鈕）[39m
[31m+   useEffect(() => {[39m
[31m+     let cancelled = false;[39m
[31m+     (async () => {[39m
[31m+       if (!order?.accommodation_id) return;[39m
[31m+       const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();[39m
[31m+       if (!user) {[39m
[31m+         if (!cancelled) setIsAdminUser(false);[39m
[31m+         return;[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       const { data } = await supabase[39m
[31m+         .from("user_roles")[39m
[31m+         .select("role, accommodation_id")[39m
[31m+         .eq("user_id", user.id);[39m
[31m+       const rows = (data ?? []) as { role: string; accommodation_id: string | null }[];[39m
[31m+       const scopedRoles = rows[39m
[31m+         .filter(r => r.accommodation_id === order.accommodation_id || r.accommodation_id === null)[39m
[31m+         .map(r => r.role);[39m
[31m+       // super_admin 為總部級角色，於任一館別（或全域）持有即視為所有館別的 super_admin[39m
[31m+       const isSuper = rows.some(r => r.role === "super_admin");[39m
[31m+       if (!cancelled) {[39m
[31m+         setIsAdminUser(isSuper || scopedRoles.includes("hotel_admin"));[39m
[31m+         setIsSuperAdmin(isSuper);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     })();[39m
[31m+     return () => { cancelled = true; };[39m
[31m+   }, [order?.accommodation_id]);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // 刪除訂單（僅 super_admin，透過後端 RPC 二次驗證權限）[39m
[31m+   const handleDeleteOrder = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     setIsDeletingOrder(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase.rpc("admin_delete_order", {[39m
[31m+         _order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         _reason: deleteReason.trim() || null,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "訂單已刪除",[39m
[31m+         description: `訂單 ${order.order_number} 已永久刪除，並已寫入稽核紀錄。`,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       setShowDeleteDialog(false);[39m
[31m+       navigate(-1);[39m
[31m+     } catch (e: any) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "刪除失敗",[39m
[31m+         description: e?.message || "無法刪除訂單，請稍後再試。",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setIsDeletingOrder(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleRepushOwlting = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     // 由確認彈窗觸發，這裡不再使用 window.confirm[39m
[31m+     setIsRepushingOwlting(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const echoToken = `RENKE-${order.order_number}-manual-${Date.now()}`;[39m
[31m+       const { error } = await supabase.from("owlting_order_push_queue").insert({[39m
[31m+         accommodation_id: order.accommodation_id,[39m
[31m+         order_id: order.id,[39m
[31m+         res_status: "Modify",[39m
[31m+         echo_token: echoToken,[39m
[31m+         status: "pending",[39m
[31m+         next_retry_at: new Date().toISOString(),[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (error) throw error;[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "已排入重新推送",[39m
[31m+         description: "任務已進入奧丁丁推送佇列，將於下一輪 cron（約 1 分鐘內）執行。",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } catch (e: any) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "重新推送失敗",[39m
[31m+         description: e?.message ?? String(e),[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setIsRepushingOwlting(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   const handleSyncTapPayRefund = async () => {[39m
[31m+     if (!order) return;[39m
[31m+     setIsSyncingRefund(true);[39m
[31m+     try {[39m
[31m+       const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("tappay-sync-refund", {[39m
[31m+         body: { orderId: order.id },[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       if (error) {[39m
[31m+         const msg = (error as any)?.context?.error ?? error.message ?? "同步失敗";[39m
[31m+         throw new Error(msg);[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (!data?.success) {[39m
[31m+         throw new Error(data?.error || "同步失敗");[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+       if (data.changed) {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "已同步 TapPay 退款狀態",[39m
[31m+           description: `本次新增退款 NT$${Number(data.delta).toLocaleString()}，付款狀態：${data.new_payment_status}`,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         // 重新載入訂單資料[39m
[31m+         window.location.reload();[39m
[31m+       } else {[39m
[31m+         toast({[39m
[31m+           title: "已是最新狀態",[39m
[31m+           description: data.message || "TapPay 無新退款記錄",[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }[39m
[31m+     } catch (e: any) {[39m
[31m+       toast({[39m
[31m+         title: "同步失敗",[39m
[31m+         description: e?.message || "請稍後再試",[39m
[31m+         variant: "destructive",[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+     } finally {[39m
[31m+       setIsSyncingRefund(false);[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   if (loading) {[39m
[31m+     return ([39m

[31m+                                   .filter(r => r.id !== or.id && r.room_unit_id && r.room_type_id === or.room_type_id)[39m
[31m+                                   .map(r => r.room_unit_id as string)[39m
[31m+                               );[39m
[31m+                               const freeUnits = allUnitsOfType.filter(u => !u.occupied_by_order_id && !siblingPicked.has(u.id));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                               // quantity > 1 → 無法套用自動安排[39m
[31m+                               if (or.quantity !== 1) {[39m
[31m+                                 return ([39m
[31m+                                   <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                                     <Label className="text-xs">指派{unitNoun}</Label>[39m
[31m+                                     <div className="h-8 px-2 rounded-md border border-dashed bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 text-[11px] flex items-center">[39m
[31m+                                       多{unitNoun}訂單請拆為多列以使用自動安排[39m
[31m+                                     </div>[39m
[31m+                                   </div>[39m
[31m+                                 );[39m
[31m+                               }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                               if (totalUnits === 0) {[39m
[31m+                                 return ([39m
[31m+                                   <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                                     <Label className="text-xs">指派{unitNoun}</Label>[39m
[31m+                                     <div className="h-8 px-2 rounded-md border border-dashed bg-muted text-muted-foreground text-[11px] flex items-center">[39m

[31m+                                     </div>[39m
[31m+                                   </div>[39m
[31m+                                 );[39m
[31m+                               }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                               const remaining = freeUnits.length;[39m
[31m+                               const isAuto = !or.room_unit_id;[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                               return ([39m
[31m+                                 <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                                   <Label className="text-xs">指派{unitNoun}</Label>[39m

[31m+                                     value={or.room_unit_id || "none"}[39m
[31m+                                     onValueChange={(v) => setEditOrderRooms(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === or.id ? { ...r, room_unit_id: v === "none" ? null : v } : r))}[39m

[31m+                                     <SelectTrigger className="h-8 text-xs">[39m
[31m+                                       <SelectValue placeholder="自動安排" />[39m

[31m+                                       <SelectItem value="none">自動安排</SelectItem>[39m

[31m+                                         const occupied = u.occupied_by_order_id && u.id !== or.room_unit_id;[39m
[31m+                                         return ([39m

[31m+                                             {u.unit_number}{u.nickname ? ` · ${u.nickname}` : ""}[39m

[31m+                                     </SelectContent>[39m
[31m+                                   </Select>[39m
[31m+                                   {isAuto && ([39m
[31m+                                     <div className={`text-[11px] leading-tight ${remaining === 0 ? "text-destructive" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>[39m
[31m+                                       {remaining === 0 ? ([39m
[31m+                                         <>[39m
[31m+                                           ⚠️ 自動安排無可用{unitNoun}（總 {totalUnits}／被占用 {occupiedByOthers}／本單已用 {siblingPicked.size}）[39m
[31m+                                           {occupiedList.length > 0 && ([39m
[31m+                                             <div className="mt-0.5">[39m
[31m+                                               占用明細：{occupiedList.slice(0, 4).map(u => `${u.unit_number}→#${u.occupied_by_order_number}`).join("、")}[39m
[31m+                                               {occupiedList.length > 4 ? ` …+${occupiedList.length - 4}` : ""}[39m
[31m+                                             </div>[39m
[31m+                                           )}[39m
[31m+                                         </>[39m
[31m+                                       ) : ([39m
[31m+                                         <>剩餘可用 {remaining}／{totalUnits} {unitNoun}　將自動指派：{freeUnits[0]?.unit_number}{freeUnits[0]?.nickname ? ` · ${freeUnits[0].nickname}` : ""}</>[39m
[31m+                                       )}[39m
[31m+                                     </div>[39m
[31m+                                   )}[39m
[31m+                                 </div>[39m

[31m+                                 inputClassName="h-8 text-xs"[39m
[31m+                                 value={or.room_price}[39m
[31m+                                 onChange={(newPrice) => {[39m
[31m+                                   setEditOrderRooms(prev => prev.map(r => r.id === or.id ? { ...r, room_price: newPrice, unit_price: r.quantity > 0 ? Math.round(newPrice / r.quantity) : newPrice } : r));[39m
[31m+                                 }}[39m
[31m+                               />[39m
[31m+                             </div>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                           {showExclusiveConfig && ([39m
[31m+                             <div className="p-3 rounded-lg border border-dashed bg-muted/20 space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                               <div className="text-xs font-medium">{exclusiveLabel}房間配置</div>[39m
[31m+                               <div className={`grid gap-2 ${rowBreakdownItems.length >= 3 ? "grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3" : "grid-cols-2"}`}>[39m
[31m+                                 {rowBreakdownItems.map(b => {[39m
[31m+                                   const val = Number(cfg[b.name]) || 0;[39m
[31m+                                   const opts = Array.from({ length: b.max + 1 }, (_, i) => i);[39m

[31m+                                     <div key={b.name} className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+                                       <Label className="text-xs">{b.name}間數</Label>[39m

[31m+                                         <SelectTrigger className="h-8 text-xs"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>[39m

[31m+                                 將以備註形式儲存：{rowBreakdownItems.map(b => `${b.name} ${Number(cfg[b.name]) || 0} 間`).join("、")}[39m

[31m+                             </div>[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                           );[39m
[31m+                         });[39m
[31m+                       })()}[39m
[31m+                     </>[39m
[31m+                   ) : ([39m
[31m+                     <>[39m
[31m+                       {orderRooms.map((or) => {[39m
[31m+                         const savedRoomsTotal = orderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+                         const displayRoomPrice = orderRooms.length === 1 && order.total_price > 0 && savedRoomsTotal !== order.total_price[39m

[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+                 <Separator />[39m
[31m+               </>[39m
[31m+             ) : ([39m
[31m+               <>[39m

[31m+                     <Select[39m
[31m+                       value={editForm.room_type_id}[39m

[31m+                         const changed = v !== editForm.room_type_id;[39m
[31m+                         setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, room_type_id: v }));[39m

[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+                 <Separator />[39m

[31m+             )}[39m

[31m+           <CardContent className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+             {/* Multi-room price breakdown */}[39m
[31m+             {orderRooms.length > 0 && !isEditing && ([39m
[31m+               <>[39m
[31m+                 {orderRooms.map((or) => {[39m
[31m+                   const savedRoomsTotal = orderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+                   const savedAddons = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+                   const customAddonTotal = savedAddons.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.total || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+                   const dedicatedAddonTotal = Number(order.pet_plan_price || 0) + Number(order.meal_plan_total || 0);[39m
[31m+                   const addonTotal = dedicatedAddonTotal > 0 ? dedicatedAddonTotal : customAddonTotal;[39m
[31m+                   const totalIncludesAddons = Math.abs([39m
[31m+                     Number(order.final_price || 0) - (Number(order.total_price || 0) - Number(order.discount_amount || 0)),[39m

[31m+                   const roomOnlyTotal = Math.max(0, Number(order.total_price || 0) - (totalIncludesAddons ? addonTotal : 0));[39m
[31m+                   const displayRoomPrice = orderRooms.length === 1 && roomOnlyTotal > 0 && savedRoomsTotal !== roomOnlyTotal[39m
[31m+                     ? roomOnlyTotal[39m
[31m+                     : or.room_price;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div key={or.id} className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">[39m
