[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {displayRoomPrice.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 })}[39m
[31m+               </>[39m
[31m+             )}[39m
[31m+             {(() => {[39m
[31m+               const savedRoomsTotal = orderRooms.reduce((sum, r) => sum + r.room_price, 0);[39m
[31m+               const savedAddons = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+               const customAddonTotal = savedAddons.reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.total || 0), 0);[39m
[31m+               const dedicatedAddonTotal = Number(order.pet_plan_price || 0) + Number(order.meal_plan_total || 0);[39m
[31m+               const addonTotal = dedicatedAddonTotal > 0 ? dedicatedAddonTotal : customAddonTotal;[39m
[31m+               const totalIncludesAddons = Math.abs([39m
[31m+                 Number(order.final_price || 0) - (Number(order.total_price || 0) - Number(order.discount_amount || 0)),[39m
[31m+               ) <= 1;[39m
[31m+               const roomOnlyTotal = Math.max(0, Number(order.total_price || 0) - (totalIncludesAddons ? addonTotal : 0));[39m
[31m+               const displayTotal = orderRooms.length === 1 && roomOnlyTotal > 0 && savedRoomsTotal !== roomOnlyTotal[39m
[31m+                 ? roomOnlyTotal[39m
[31m+                 : (orderRooms.length > 0 ? savedRoomsTotal : roomOnlyTotal);[39m
[31m+               // 編輯模式始終顯示；檢視模式下僅在無 orderRooms 或多房型時顯示原始金額[39m
[31m+               const showInViewMode = orderRooms.length !== 1;[39m
[31m+               if (!isEditing && !showInViewMode) return null;[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <Separator />[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">原始金額</span>[39m
[31m+                     {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+                       <DualCurrencyInput config={secondaryCurrencyConfig} rawMode={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} storageKey={order.accommodation_id} forcedMode={currencyForcedMode} onModeChange={handleCurrencyModeChange} hideToggle={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} className="w-[150px]" inputClassName="h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm" disabled={!isSuperAdmin && ["paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status)} value={editForm.total_price} onChange={(v) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, total_price: v }))} />[39m
[31m+                     ) : ([39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {displayTotal.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             })()}[39m
[31m+             {/* 折扣金額與折扣碼 */}[39m
[31m+             <Separator />[39m
[31m+             <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+               <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">折扣金額</span>[39m
[31m+               {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+                 <DualCurrencyInput config={secondaryCurrencyConfig} rawMode={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} storageKey={order.accommodation_id} forcedMode={currencyForcedMode} onModeChange={handleCurrencyModeChange} hideToggle={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} className="w-[150px]" inputClassName="h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm" disabled={["paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status) && !INLINE_PAID_DISCOUNT_EDITABLE} value={editForm.discount_amount} onChange={(v) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, discount_amount: v }))} />[39m
[31m+               ) : ([39m
[31m+                 order.discount_amount > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base text-destructive">- {orderCurrencyPrefix} {order.discount_amount.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                 ) : ([39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} 0</span>[39m
[31m+                 )[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             <div className="space-y-1">[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">折扣碼</span>[39m
[31m+                 {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+                   <Input className="w-[140px] h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm text-right uppercase" type="text" placeholder="選填" disabled={["paid", "fully_paid"].includes(order.payment_status) && !INLINE_PAID_DISCOUNT_EDITABLE} value={editForm.discount_code} onChange={(e) => { setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, discount_code: e.target.value.toUpperCase() })); setDiscountCodeError(""); }} />[39m
[31m+                 ) : ([39m
[31m+                   order.discount_code ? ([39m
[31m+                     <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{order.discount_code}</Badge>[39m
[31m+                   ) : ([39m
[31m+                     <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base text-muted-foreground">—</span>[39m
[31m+                   )[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               {isEditing && discountCodeError && ([39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-xs text-destructive text-right">{discountCodeError}</p>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+             {/* 加購方案金額 - 編輯模式顯示匯總輸入框 */}[39m
[31m+             {isEditing && (() => {[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <Separator />[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">加購小計</span>[39m
[31m+                     <DualCurrencyInput disabled={isPaidAddonFlowOrder(order as any)} config={secondaryCurrencyConfig} rawMode={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} storageKey={order.accommodation_id} forcedMode={currencyForcedMode} onModeChange={handleCurrencyModeChange} hideToggle={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} className="w-[150px]" inputClassName="h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm" value={editForm.addon_total} onChange={(v) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, addon_total: v }))} />[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   {isPaidAddonFlowOrder(order as any) && ([39m
[31m+                     <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">[39m
[31m+                       已結清訂單的折扣與額外加購請使用價格明細上方的「修改已結清折扣」／「修改額外加購」，此處欄位不會被儲存。[39m
[31m+                     </p>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">額外現場加購金額</span>[39m
[31m+                     <DualCurrencyInput[39m
[31m+                       disabled={isPaidAddonFlowOrder(order as any)}[39m
[31m+                       config={secondaryCurrencyConfig}[39m
[31m+                       rawMode={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig}[39m
[31m+                       storageKey={order.accommodation_id} forcedMode={currencyForcedMode} onModeChange={handleCurrencyModeChange} hideToggle={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig}[39m
[31m+                       className="w-[150px]"[39m
[31m+                       inputClassName="h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm"[39m
[31m+                       placeholder="0"[39m
[31m+                       value={editForm.extra_addon_amount || 0}[39m
[31m+                       onChange={(v) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, extra_addon_amount: v }))}[39m
[31m+                     />[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">額外現場加購備註</span>[39m
[31m+                     <Textarea[39m
[31m+                       disabled={isPaidAddonFlowOrder(order as any)}[39m
[31m+                       className="min-h-[60px] text-base md:text-sm"[39m
[31m+                       placeholder="例如：加購早餐2份500元、額外床墊1張800元"[39m
[31m+                       value={editForm.addon_note}[39m
[31m+                       onChange={(e) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, addon_note: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+                     />[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             })()}[39m
[31m+             {/* 加購明細 - 檢視模式逐項顯示 */}[39m
[31m+             {!isEditing && (() => {[39m
[31m+               const customAddons = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+               const items: { label: string; amount: number }[] = [];[39m
[31m+               if (order.pet_plan_price > 0) {[39m
[31m+                 items.push({ label: `寵物加購 (${order.pet_count || 1}隻)`, amount: order.pet_plan_price });[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+               if (order.meal_plan_total > 0) {[39m
[31m+                 items.push({ label: '餐食加購', amount: order.meal_plan_total });[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+               customAddons.forEach(d => {[39m
[31m+                 if (d.total > 0) {[39m
[31m+                   items.push({ label: `${d.addon_type} (${d.count} × ${d.unit_label || '份'})`, amount: d.total });[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+               });[39m
[31m+               const _notesRaw = order.notes || "";[39m
[31m+               const _extraMatch = _notesRaw.match(/【額外現場加購金額[^】]*】\n?(\d+)\n?【額外現場加購金額END】/);[39m
[31m+               const extraAmount = _extraMatch ? (parseInt(_extraMatch[1], 10) || 0) : 0;[39m
[31m+               if (extraAmount > 0) {[39m
[31m+                 items.push({ label: '額外現場加購金額', amount: extraAmount });[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+               const _blocks = [..._notesRaw.matchAll(/【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)[^】]*】\n?([\s\S]*?)\n?【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)END】/g)];[39m
[31m+               const _lineMatch = _notesRaw.match(/^【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)[^】]*】(.*)$/m);[39m
[31m+               const addonNote = _blocks.length > 0 ? _blocks[_blocks.length - 1][1].trim() : (_lineMatch ? _lineMatch[1].trim() : "");[39m
[31m+               if (items.length === 0 && !addonNote) return null;[39m
[31m+               const addonTotal = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.amount, 0);[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <Separator />[39m
[31m+                   {items.map((item, idx) => ([39m
[31m+                     <div key={idx} className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">{item.label}</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {item.amount.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   ))}[39m
[31m+                   {addonNote && ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2 pt-1">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">額外現場加購備註</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-right whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{addonNote}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                   {items.length > 1 && ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pt-1">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">加購小計</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {addonTotal.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   )}[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             })()}[39m
[31m+             <Separator />[39m
[31m+             {/* 實收金額 — 與訂單列表一致，直接讀取 orders.final_price */}[39m
[31m+             {(() => {[39m
[31m+               const effectiveFinalPrice = order.final_price;[39m
[31m+               return ([39m
[31m+                 <>[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-start gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-semibold">實收金額</span>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex flex-col items-end leading-tight">[39m
[31m+                       <span className={`font-bold text-lg md:text-lg ${isEditing && (editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total - editForm.discount_amount) < 0 ? 'text-destructive' : ''}`}>[39m
[31m+                         {orderCurrencyPrefix} {isEditing ? (editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total - editForm.discount_amount).toLocaleString() : effectiveFinalPrice.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                       {secondaryCurrencyConfig && !isRawCurrency && ([39m
[31m+                         <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">[39m
[31m+                           ≈ {formatSecondary([39m
[31m+                             isEditing[39m
[31m+                               ? (editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total - editForm.discount_amount)[39m
[31m+                               : effectiveFinalPrice,[39m
[31m+                             secondaryCurrencyConfig[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                         </span>[39m
[31m+                       )}[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                    <Separator />[39m
[31m+                   {/* 匯款（含舊訂單）：顯示訂金/尾款拆分與各自付款狀態 */}[39m
[31m+                   {(() => {[39m
[31m+                     const method = (order as any).balance_payment_method || (order as any).deposit_payment_method || (order as any).payment_method;[39m
[31m+                     const dep = isEditing ? (editForm.deposit_amount ?? 0) : (order.deposit_amount ?? 0);[39m
[31m+                     const finalDisplay = isEditing[39m
[31m+                       ? (editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total - editForm.discount_amount)[39m
[31m+                       : (order.final_price ?? 0);[39m
[31m+                     const isBankTransfer = method === 'bank_transfer';[39m
[31m+                     const isLegacySplit = (order.deposit_amount ?? 0) > 0 && (order.deposit_amount ?? 0) < (order.final_price ?? 0);[39m
[31m+                     if (!isBankTransfer && !isLegacySplit) return null;[39m
[31m+                     const paid = isEditing ? editForm.paid_amount : (order.paid_amount ?? 0);[39m
[31m+                     const depositPaid = order.payment_status === 'deposit_paid' || order.payment_status === 'paid' || (paid >= dep && dep > 0);[39m
[31m+                     const balancePaid = order.payment_status === 'paid' || paid >= finalDisplay;[39m
[31m+                     const canEditSplit = isEditing;[39m
[31m+                     const balance = Math.max(0, finalDisplay - dep);[39m
[31m+                     return ([39m
[31m+                       <>[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">訂金</span>[39m
[31m+                           {canEditSplit ? ([39m
[31m+                             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                               <Input[39m
[31m+                                 className="w-[140px] h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm text-right"[39m
[31m+                                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                                 inputMode="numeric"[39m
[31m+                                 value={dep}[39m
[31m+                                 onChange={(e) => {[39m
[31m+                                   const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(finalDisplay, parseInt(e.target.value) || 0));[39m
[31m+                                   depositRatioRef.current = finalDisplay > 0 ? v / finalDisplay : 0;[39m
[31m+                                   setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, deposit_amount: v }));[39m
[31m+                                 }}[39m
[31m+                               />[39m
[31m+                               <span className={`text-xs ${depositPaid ? 'text-primary' : 'text-destructive'}`}>[39m
[31m+                                 {depositPaid ? '已付' : '未付'}[39m
[31m+                               </span>[39m
[31m+                             </div>[39m
[31m+                           ) : ([39m
[31m+                             <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">[39m
[31m+                               {orderCurrencyPrefix} {dep.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+                               <span className={`ml-2 text-xs ${depositPaid ? 'text-primary' : 'text-destructive'}`}>[39m
[31m+                                 {depositPaid ? '已付' : '未付'}[39m
[31m+                               </span>[39m
[31m+                             </span>[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <Separator />[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">尾款</span>[39m
[31m+                           {canEditSplit ? ([39m
[31m+                             <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                               <Input[39m
[31m+                                 className="w-[140px] h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm text-right"[39m
[31m+                                 type="text"[39m
[31m+                                 inputMode="numeric"[39m
[31m+                                 value={balance}[39m
[31m+                                 onChange={(e) => {[39m
[31m+                                   const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(finalDisplay, parseInt(e.target.value) || 0));[39m
[31m+                                   const newDep = Math.max(0, finalDisplay - v);[39m
[31m+                                   depositRatioRef.current = finalDisplay > 0 ? newDep / finalDisplay : 0;[39m
[31m+                                   setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, deposit_amount: newDep }));[39m
[31m+                                 }}[39m
[31m+                               />[39m
[31m+                               <span className={`text-xs ${balancePaid ? 'text-primary' : 'text-destructive'}`}>[39m
[31m+                                 {balancePaid ? '已付' : '未付'}[39m
[31m+                               </span>[39m
[31m+                             </div>[39m
[31m+                           ) : ([39m
[31m+                             <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">[39m
[31m+                               {orderCurrencyPrefix} {balance.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+                               <span className={`ml-2 text-xs ${balancePaid ? 'text-primary' : 'text-destructive'}`}>[39m
[31m+                                 {balancePaid ? '已付' : '未付'}[39m
[31m+                               </span>[39m
[31m+                             </span>[39m
[31m+                           )}[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <Separator />[39m
[31m+                       </>[39m
[31m+                     );[39m
[31m+                   })()}[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">已付金額</span>[39m
[31m+                     {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+                       <DualCurrencyInput config={secondaryCurrencyConfig} rawMode={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} storageKey={order.accommodation_id} forcedMode={currencyForcedMode} onModeChange={handleCurrencyModeChange} hideToggle={!!secondaryCurrencyConfig} className="w-[150px]" inputClassName="h-10 text-base md:h-8 md:text-sm" value={editForm.paid_amount} onChange={(v) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, paid_amount: v }))} />[39m
[31m+                     ) : ([39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base text-primary">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {order.paid_amount.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                     )}[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                   <Separator />[39m
[31m+                   <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                     <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">待付金額</span>[39m
[31m+                     <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base text-destructive">[39m
[31m+                       {orderCurrencyPrefix} {isEditing[39m
[31m+                         ? ((editForm.total_price + editForm.addon_total - editForm.discount_amount) - editForm.paid_amount).toLocaleString()[39m
[31m+                         : (effectiveFinalPrice - order.paid_amount).toLocaleString()[39m
[31m+                       }[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             })()}[39m
[31m+           </CardContent>[39m
[31m+         </Card>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* ATM 虛擬帳號資訊 */}[39m
[31m+       {(() => {[39m
[31m+         const o = order as any;[39m
[31m+         if (!o?.atm_vacc_no) return null;[39m
[31m+         const expireAt = o.atm_expire_at ? new Date(o.atm_expire_at) : null;[39m
[31m+         // 已付訂金／已付清的訂單不視為逾期（與訂單列表狀態一致）[39m
[31m+         const isExpired = isAtmExpired(o);[39m
[31m+         const isAtmPaid = o.payment_status === "paid" || o.payment_status === "deposit_paid";[39m
[31m+         const copyToClipboard = async (text: string, label: string) => {[39m
[31m+           try {[39m
[31m+             await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);[39m
[31m+             toast({ title: "已複製", description: `${label}已複製到剪貼簿` });[39m
[31m+           } catch {[39m
[31m+             toast({ title: "複製失敗", variant: "destructive" });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+         return ([39m
[31m+           <Card>[39m
[31m+             <CardHeader className="pb-3">[39m
[31m+               <CardTitle className="text-lg md:text-lg flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-primary" />[39m
[31m+                 ATM 虛擬帳號[39m
[31m+                 {isExpired ? ([39m
[31m+                   <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-destructive border-destructive/30">已逾期</Badge>[39m
[31m+                 ) : isAtmPaid ? ([39m
[31m+                   <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-primary border-primary/30">[39m
[31m+                     {o.payment_status === "paid" ? "已付清" : "已付訂金"}[39m
[31m+                   </Badge>[39m
[31m+                 ) : ([39m
[31m+                   <Badge variant="outline" className="ml-auto text-primary border-primary/30">待付款</Badge>[39m
[31m+                 )}[39m
[31m+               </CardTitle>[39m
[31m+             </CardHeader>[39m
[31m+             <CardContent className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+               {o.payment_method === "atm_payuni" && (() => {[39m
[31m+                 const dep = Number(o.booking_deposit_amount || 0);[39m
[31m+                 const paid = Number(o.paid_amount || 0);[39m
[31m+                 const balance = Math.max(0, Number(o.final_price || 0) - Math.max(paid, dep));[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <div className="rounded-md border bg-muted/30 px-3 py-2 text-sm" data-testid="payuni-atm-order-summary">[39m
[31m+                     PAYUNi ATM 訂金 {paid > 0 ? "已付" : "未付"}（NT$ {dep.toLocaleString()}）{balance > 0 ? `・尾款現場收 NT$ ${balance.toLocaleString()}` : ""}[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               })()}[39m
[31m+               <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                 <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">銀行代碼</span>[39m
[31m+                 <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                   <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-base">{o.atm_vacc_bank_code || "—"}</span>[39m

[31m+                     <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="h-7 w-7 p-0" onClick={() => copyToClipboard(o.atm_vacc_bank_code, "銀行代碼")}>[39m
[31m+                       <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />[39m
[31m+                     </Button>[39m

[31m+               </div>[39m
[31m+               <Separator />[39m

[31m+                 <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">虛擬帳號</span>[39m

[31m+               </div>[39m

[31m+                       {isExpired ? "（已逾期）" : ""}[39m
[31m+                     </span>[39m
[31m+                   </div>[39m
[31m+                 </>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             </CardContent>[39m
[31m+           </Card>[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+       })()}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* 押金管理 */}[39m
[31m+       {order && <DepositManagementSection orderId={order.id} />}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* 加購方案 */}[39m
[31m+       <Card>[39m
[31m+         <CardHeader className="pb-3">[39m
[31m+           <CardTitle className="text-lg md:text-lg">加購方案</CardTitle>[39m
[31m+         </CardHeader>[39m
[31m+         <CardContent className="space-y-3">[39m
[31m+           {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+             <>[39m
[31m+               {/* 寵物方案 - 編輯模式 */}[39m
[31m+               {(() => {[39m
[31m+                 const petAddon = addonSettings.find(a => a.addon_type === 'pet');[39m
[31m+                 return petAddon ? ([39m

[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-medium">🐾 {petAddon.description || '寵物方案'}</span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pl-2 md:pl-4">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">數量（{petAddon.unit_label}）</span>[39m
[31m+                       <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m

[31m+                             setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, pet_count: count, pet_plan_price: count * petAddon.price_per_unit * nights }));[39m

[31m+                             const count = Math.min(petAddon.max_quantity, editForm.pet_count + 1);[39m
[31m+                             setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, pet_count: count, pet_plan_price: count * petAddon.price_per_unit * nights }));[39m

[31m+                     </div>[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {/* 其他自訂加購方案 - 編輯模式 */}[39m
[31m+               {(() => {[39m
[31m+                 const genericAddons = addonSettings.filter(a => a.addon_type !== 'pet' && a.addon_type !== 'meal' && a.addon_type !== 'bbq');[39m
[31m+                 return genericAddons.map((addon, idx) => {[39m
[31m+                   const matchesAddon = (d: CustomAddonDetail) =>[39m
[31m+                     d.addon_id ? d.addon_id === addon.id : d.addon_type === addon.addon_type;[39m
[31m+                   const existing = editForm.custom_addon_details.find(matchesAddon);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                   const count = existing?.count || 0;[39m
[31m+                   const total = existing?.total || 0;[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <React.Fragment key={addon.addon_type}>[39m
[31m+                       {idx > 0 && <Separator />}[39m
[31m+                       <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-medium">📦 {addon.addon_type}</span>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pl-2 md:pl-4">[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">數量（{addon.unit_label}）</span>[39m
[31m+                           <div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                             <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7" disabled={count <= 0}[39m
[31m+                               onClick={() => {[39m
[31m+                                 const newCount = addonStepDown(count, addon.min_quantity);[39m
[31m+                                 const newTotal = newCount * addon.price_per_unit * addonNightsMultiplier(addon.pricing_mode, nights);[39m
[31m+                                 const newDetails = editForm.custom_addon_details.filter(d => !matchesAddon(d));[39m
[31m+                                 if (newCount > 0) {[39m
[31m+                                   newDetails.push({ addon_id: addon.id, addon_type: addon.addon_type, count: newCount, price_per_unit: addon.price_per_unit, total: newTotal, unit_label: addon.unit_label });[39m
[31m+                                 }[39m
[31m+                                 setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, custom_addon_details: newDetails }));[39m
[31m+                               }}>[39m
[31m+                               <Minus className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                             </Button>[39m
[31m+                             <span className="w-8 text-center text-sm font-medium">{count}</span>[39m
[31m+                             <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7" disabled={count >= addon.max_quantity}[39m
[31m+                               onClick={() => {[39m
[31m+                                 const newCount = addonStepUp(count, addon.min_quantity, addon.max_quantity);[39m
[31m+                                 const newTotal = newCount * addon.price_per_unit * addonNightsMultiplier(addon.pricing_mode, nights);[39m
[31m+                                 const newDetails = editForm.custom_addon_details.filter(d => !matchesAddon(d));[39m
[31m+                                 if (newCount > 0) {[39m
[31m+                                   newDetails.push({ addon_id: addon.id, addon_type: addon.addon_type, count: newCount, price_per_unit: addon.price_per_unit, total: newTotal, unit_label: addon.unit_label });[39m
[31m+                                 }[39m
[31m+                                 setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, custom_addon_details: newDetails }));[39m
[31m+                               }}>[39m
[31m+                               <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />[39m
[31m+                             </Button>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                         <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pl-2 md:pl-4">[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">單價</span>[39m
[31m+                           <span className="text-base md:text-sm">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {addon.price_per_unit.toLocaleString()} / {addon.unit_label} {addonPricingSuffix(addon.pricing_mode)}</span>[39m

[31m+                           <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">小計</span>[39m
[31m+                           <span className="font-medium text-sm">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {total.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                       </div>[39m
[31m+                     </React.Fragment>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 });[39m
[31m+               })()}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {(() => {[39m
[31m+                 const customTotal = editForm.custom_addon_details.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0);[39m
[31m+                 const grandTotal = editForm.pet_plan_price + editForm.meal_plan_total + customTotal;[39m
[31m+                 return grandTotal > 0 ? ([39m
[31m+                   <>[39m
[31m+                     <Separator />[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pt-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-semibold">加購總計</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-bold text-lg md:text-lg text-primary">[39m
[31m+                         {orderCurrencyPrefix} {grandTotal.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </>[39m
[31m+                 ) : null;[39m
[31m+               })()}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               {editForm.pet_count === 0 && editForm.meal_plan_details.length === 0 && editForm.custom_addon_details.length === 0 && addonSettings.filter(a => a.addon_type !== 'pet' && a.addon_type !== 'meal' && a.addon_type !== 'bbq').length === 0 && addonSettings.length === 0 && ([39m
[31m+                 <div className="text-center py-4">[39m
[31m+                   <p className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">無可用的加購方案</p>[39m
[31m+                 </div>[39m
[31m+               )}[39m
[31m+             </>[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             <>[39m
[31m+               {(() => {[39m
[31m+                 const customAddons = (order.custom_addon_details as CustomAddonDetail[]) || [];[39m
[31m+                 const hasAnyAddon = order.pet_count > 0 || order.meal_plan_total > 0 || customAddons.length > 0;[39m
[31m+                 if (!hasAnyAddon) {[39m
[31m+                   return ([39m
[31m+                     <div className="text-center py-4 md:py-6">[39m
[31m+                       <p className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">此訂單無加購方案</p>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   );[39m
[31m+                 }[39m
[31m+                 const customTotal = customAddons.reduce((sum, d) => sum + d.total, 0);[39m
[31m+                 const grandTotal = order.pet_plan_price + order.meal_plan_total + customTotal;[39m
[31m+                 return ([39m
[31m+                   <>[39m
[31m+                     {order.pet_count > 0 && ([39m
[31m+                       <>[39m
[31m+                         <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                           <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m
[31m+                             <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-medium">🐾 寵物方案</span>[39m
[31m+                             <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs">{order.pet_count} 隻 × {nights} 晚</Badge>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                           <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pl-2 md:pl-4">[39m
[31m+                             <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">小計</span>[39m
[31m+                             <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {order.pet_plan_price.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                       </>[39m

[31m+                     {order.meal_plan_total > 0 && order.meal_plan_details && order.meal_plan_details.length > 0 && ([39m

[31m+                           <div className="pl-2 md:pl-4 space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                             {order.meal_plan_details.map((detail, index) => ([39m
[31m+                               <div key={index} className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 text-sm md:text-sm">[39m
[31m+                                 <span className="text-muted-foreground flex-shrink-0">[39m
[31m+                                   {format(new Date(detail.date), "yyyy/MM/dd")} - {detail.count} 份[39m
[31m+                                 </span>[39m
[31m+                                 <span className="font-medium">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {detail.price.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                               </div>[39m

[31m+                             </div>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+                     {customAddons.map((addon, idx) => ([39m
[31m+                       <React.Fragment key={addon.addon_type}>[39m
[31m+                         {(idx > 0 || order.pet_count > 0 || order.meal_plan_total > 0) && <Separator />}[39m
[31m+                         <div className="space-y-2">[39m
[31m+                           <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2">[39m

[31m+                           <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pl-2 md:pl-4">[39m
[31m+                             <span className="text-sm md:text-sm text-muted-foreground">小計</span>[39m
[31m+                             <span className="font-medium text-base md:text-base">{orderCurrencyPrefix} {addon.total.toLocaleString()}</span>[39m
[31m+                           </div>[39m
[31m+                         </div>[39m
[31m+                       </React.Fragment>[39m
[31m+                     ))}[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+                     <Separator />[39m
[31m+                     <div className="flex justify-between items-center gap-2 pt-2">[39m
[31m+                       <span className="text-sm md:text-sm font-semibold">加購總計</span>[39m
[31m+                       <span className="font-bold text-lg md:text-lg text-primary">[39m
[31m+                         {orderCurrencyPrefix} {grandTotal.toLocaleString()}[39m
[31m+                       </span>[39m
[31m+                     </div>[39m
[31m+                   </>[39m

[31m+               })()}[39m
[31m+             </>[39m
[31m+           )}[39m
[31m+         </CardContent>[39m
[31m+       </Card>[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+       {/* 備註 */}[39m
[31m+       <Card>[39m
[31m+         <CardHeader className="pb-3">[39m
[31m+           <CardTitle className="text-lg md:text-lg">訂單備註</CardTitle>[39m
[31m+         </CardHeader>[39m
[31m+         <CardContent>[39m
[31m+           {isEditing ? ([39m
[31m+             <Textarea[39m
[31m+               value={editForm.notes}[39m
[31m+               onChange={(e) => setEditForm(f => ({ ...f, notes: e.target.value }))}[39m
[31m+               rows={4}[39m
[31m+               placeholder="輸入備註..."[39m
[31m+               className="text-base md:text-sm"[39m
[31m+             />[39m
[31m+           ) : ([39m
[31m+             (() => {[39m
[31m+               // 顯示時：保留加購備註內容（含標題），僅移除技術用 END 標記[39m
[31m+               const cleaned = (order.notes || "")[39m
[31m+                 .replace(/【額外現場加購金額[^】]*】\n?[\s\S]*?\n?【額外現場加購金額END】\n?/g, "")[39m
[31m+                 .replace(/\n?【(?:加購備註|額外現場加購備註)END】\n?/g, "\n")[39m
[31m+                 .replace(/\n{3,}/g, "\n\n")[39m
[31m+                 .trim();[39m
[31m+               return cleaned ? ([39m
[31m+                 <p className="text-sm md:text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">{cleaned}</p>[39m
[31m+               ) : ([39m

[31m+               <Textarea[39m

[31m+                 placeholder="請填寫修改金額的原因..."[39m
[31m+                 value={editReason}[39m
[31m+                 onChange={(e) => setEditReason(e.target.value)}[39m
[31m+                 maxLength={500}[39m
[31m+                 rows={3}[39m
[31m+               />[39m
[31m+             </div>[39m
[31m+           </div>[39m
[31m+           <DialogFooter>[39m
[31m+             <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setShowPriceEditDialog(false)}>取消</Button>[39m
[31m+             <Button onClick={handleSavePriceEdit} disabled={isSavingPrice}>[39m
[31m+               {isSavingPrice ? "儲存中..." : "確認修改"}[39m
[31m+             </Button>[39m
[31m+           </DialogFooter>[39m
[31m+         </DialogContent>[39m
[31m+       </Dialog>[39m
[31m+       {isSuperAdmin && ([39m
[31m+         <CommissionEligibilityExceptionDialog[39m
[31m+           orderId={showMetaDrawer ? order.id : null}[39m
[31m+           open={showMetaDrawer}[39m
[31m+           onClose={() => setShowMetaDrawer(false)}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {isSuperAdmin && ([39m
[31m+         <DealDateEditDialog[39m
[31m+           open={dealDateOpen}[39m
[31m+           onOpenChange={setDealDateOpen}[39m
[31m+           orderId={order.id}[39m
[31m+           orderNumber={order.order_number}[39m
[31m+           accommodationId={(order as any).accommodation_id ?? null}[39m
[31m+           currentOrderDate={order.order_date}[39m
[31m+           onSaved={() => { fetchOrder(); }}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+       {kolQuotaPrompt && ([39m
[31m+         <KolQuotaConfirmDialog[39m
[31m+           open[39m
[31m+           yearMonth={kolQuotaPrompt.yearMonth}[39m
[31m+           used={kolQuotaPrompt.used}[39m
[31m+           quota={kolQuotaPrompt.quota}[39m
[31m+           packageName={kolQuotaPrompt.packageName}[39m
[31m+           onCancel={() => { kolQuotaPrompt.resolve(false); setKolQuotaPrompt(null); }}[39m
[31m+           onConfirm={() => { kolQuotaPrompt.resolve(true); setKolQuotaPrompt(null); }}[39m
[31m+         />[39m
[31m+       )}[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+   );[39m
[31m+ };[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export default OrderDetailPage;[39m
